---

> Cikakken bayani na kowane environment variable da OmniRoute ke ganewa.
> Don samfurin farawa cikin sauri, duba [`.env.example`](../../.env.example).

> [!IMPORTANT]
> Dole ne kowane variable da aka rubuta a nan ya bayyana a cikin `.env.example`, kuma
> kowane variable da ke cikin `.env.example` dole ne ya bayyana a nan. `npm run check:env-doc-sync`
> yana tabbatar da wannan yayin commit da kuma a cikin CI. Don tsallake wani variable da gangan, ƙara shi zuwa
> allowlist da ke cikin `scripts/check/check-env-doc-sync.mjs`.

## 1. Sirran da Ake Buƙata

Sirran da ba a saita ba waɗanda aka yi wa alamar **Eh** ana ƙirƙirar su ta atomatik a farkon aiki (`JWT_SECRET`, `API_KEY_SECRET`) sannan a adana su a `server.env` cikin kundin bayanai — saita su kai tsaye idan wannan kundin na wucin gadi ne. Banda wannan shi ne `OMNIROUTE_WS_BRIDGE_SECRET`: ba a adana shi a `server.env` — wuraren shigar uwar garken suna ƙirƙirar ƙimar wucin gadi ta kowace kunnawa wadda gada da ke cikin tsari kawai za ta iya amfani da ita, saboda haka abokan cinikin gada na waje suna buƙatar a saita ta kai tsaye.

| Sauyi                        | Wajibi           | Tsoho                         | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                           | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| ---------------------------- | ---------------- | ----------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `JWT_SECRET`                 | **Eh**           | _(babu)_                      | `src/lib/auth`                                            | Yana sanya hannu/tabbatar da duk kukis na zaman dashboard (JWT). Ƙirƙira da `openssl rand -base64 48`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `API_KEY_SECRET`             | **Eh**           | _(babu)_                      | `src/lib/db/apiKeys.ts`                                   | Maɓallin ɓoyewar AES don ƙimomin maɓallan API da aka adana a SQLite. Ƙirƙira da `openssl rand -hex 32`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `INITIAL_PASSWORD`           | A'a              | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/auth/managementPassword.ts`                      | Yana saita kalmar sirrin dashboard lokacin farawa muddin ba a riga an adana wata kalmar sirri ba; ana yin watsi da shi bayan an adana kalmar sirri. Shigarwar npm da ta lambar tushe suna kwafe `.env.example` zuwa `.env`, don haka suna farawa da sanannen ƙimar riƙon wuri `CHANGEME`: maye gurbinta kafin kunnawa ta farko, ko saita `INITIAL_PASSWORD=` (fanko) a cikin wannan `.env` domin ƙirƙirar kalmar sirri a mayen fara amfani a ziyarar farko, kamar yadda ƙimar da ba a saita ba (tsohuwar ƙimar hoton Docker) take yi. Sauya kalmar sirri da aka adana a Dashboard → Settings → Security, ko gudanar da `omniroute reset-password` (`node bin/reset-password.mjs` a cikin kwafin lambar tushe) tare da saita `DATA_DIR` zuwa kundin bayanan uwar garken. |
| `OMNIROUTE_WS_BRIDGE_SECRET` | **Eh** (samarwa) | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `src/app/api/internal/codex-responses-ws/route.ts`        | Sirrin da aka raba don gadar WebSocket ta Codex Responses ta ciki. Yana tantance buƙatun gada tsakanin mai isar da WS na Electron/mai lilo da OmniRoute. ⚠️ **ANA BUƘATARSA a samarwa — idan ba a saita shi ba, ana ƙin duk buƙatun gadar WS.** Ƙirƙira da `openssl rand -base64 32`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_SW_BUILD_ID`      | A'a              | _(SHA na git)_                | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/build/assembleStandalone.mjs` | Takamaiman id na karya cache na service-worker don harsashin PWA (#11779); shi ne na farko a jerin tantancewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `SOURCE_VERSION`             | A'a              | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/build/assembleStandalone.mjs` | Na biyu a jerin — masu gina PaaS (misali irin na Heroku) ne ke saita shi a matsayin commit ɗin da aka tura.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_SW_BUILD_ID`    | A'a              | _(an samo)_                   | `src/shared/components/PwaRegister.tsx`                   | Ƙimar jama'a ta lokacin ginawa da abokin ciniki ke amfani da ita don yin rajistar `/sw.js?v=…`; ana samo ta daga biyun da ke sama, sannan SHA na git.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_PEER_STAMP_TOKEN` | A'a (atomatik)   | _(atomatik a kowace kunnawa)_ | `src/server/authz/policies/management.ts`                 | Sirrin kowane tsari da ke tabbatar da cewa tambarin IP na amintaccen takwara ya fito daga uwar garken HTTP ta OmniRoute (`scripts/dev/peer-stamp.mjs`). Middleware na authz yana amincewa da kasancewar buƙata a gida (takaita hanyoyin LOCAL_ONLY zuwa loopback/LAN) ne kawai idan tambarin yana ɗauke da wannan token. Ana ƙirƙirarsa ta atomatik a kowace kunnawa — bar shi ba a saita ba; saita shi tabbatacce kawai don tsarin matakai da yawa waɗanda dole ne su raba tambarin.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRUSTED_PROXIES`  | A'a              | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `scripts/dev/peer-stamp.mjs`                              | Jerin IP ko kewayon CIDR na reverse proxies da aka raba da waƙafi, waɗanda jerin yarda/ƙin IP zai iya amincewa da `X-Forwarded-For` / `X-Real-IP` nasu, ban da loopback, adireshin hanyar sadarwa mai zaman kanta da gefunan Cloudflare, waɗanda ake amincewa da su koyaushe. Ana buƙatarsa ne kawai don proxy da ke kan wani adireshin jama'a dabam; in ba haka ba, adireshin proxy ɗin kansa ne matatar za ta tantance. Takaita yawan buƙatu na kowane abokin ciniki na relay (`/api/v1/relay/chat/completions`) yana amfani da wannan adireshin abokin ciniki da aka tantance, ba kan taken turawa kaɗai ba.                                                                                                                                                         |

### Umarnin Ƙirƙirawa

```bash
# Ƙirƙiri dukkan sirran huɗu a lokaci guda:
echo "JWT_SECRET=$(openssl rand -base64 48)"
echo "API_KEY_SECRET=$(openssl rand -hex 32)"
echo "INITIAL_PASSWORD=$(openssl rand -base64 16)"
echo "OMNIROUTE_WS_BRIDGE_SECRET=$(openssl rand -base64 32)"
```

> [!CAUTION]
> Kada a taɓa commit na fayilolin `.env` masu sirri na gaske zuwa tsarin sarrafa sigogi. `.gitignore` ya riga ya ware `.env`, amma tabbatar kafin turawa.

---

## 2. Ma'ajiya & Database

OmniRoute yana amfani da **SQLite** (ta hanyar `better-sqlite3`) don duk adanawa na dindindin. Waɗannan masu canji suna sarrafa wurin bayanai, ɓoyewa, da tsarin rayuwa.

| Variable                                   | Default                       | Source File                                           | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `DATA_DIR`                                 | `~/.omniroute/`               | `src/lib/db/core.ts`                                  | Babban direktili na SQLite DB, ajiyar madadi, da fayilolin bayanai. Canza shi don Docker volumes ko hanyoyin da aka keɓance.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_DEFAULT_DATA_DIR`         | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/dataPaths.ts`                                | Hanyar kubucewa ga gadin `DATA_DIR` na test/eval (#10428). Ana mayar da gwaje-gwaje da binciken eval/print na Node (`-e`/`--eval`/`-p`/`--print`, gami da nau'ikan `--eval=`/`--print=`) waɗanda ba su da `DATA_DIR` zuwa wani direktilin wucin gadi da za a jefar domin kada su buɗe ainihin database na mai aiki; saita zuwa `1` don komawa amfani da ainihin direktilin.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_BUILD_SHA`                      | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/monitoring/buildSha.ts`                      | Git SHA na artifact da ke gudana. `npm run build:release` ne ke buga shi; ana iya shigar da shi a cikin containers da aka aika ba tare da sentinel ɗin `dist/BUILD_SHA` ba. Yana bayyana a matsayin `system.buildSha` a kan `/api/monitoring/health`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_RELEASE_REF`                    | `origin/main`                 | `scripts/build/buildProvenance.ts`                    | Ref ɗin da ƙofar provenance na pack-artifact ke kwatanta build SHA da shi (#10427).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_CANARY_BUILD`             | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `scripts/build/buildProvenance.ts`                    | Saita zuwa `1` don barin yin pack na build wanda SHA ɗinsa baya kan layin release, ana rubuta shi a matsayin canary da gangan maimakon kasa ƙofar (#10427).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_SMOKE_API_KEY`                  | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `scripts/ops/deploy-canary.mjs`                       | API key na binciken smoke na canary-deploy, ana aika shi a matsayin `Authorization: Bearer` a kan `/v1/chat/completions`. Kawai script ɗin deploy (#10429) ne ke amfani da shi, ba ma'aikatar (server) ba. Bai da alaƙa da masu canjin `OMNIROUTE_SMOKE_*` na zaɓin CLI smoke harness (`RUN_CLI_SMOKE=1`, `OMNIROUTE_SMOKE_BASE_URL/MODEL/API_KEY_ENV/TARGETS/TIMEOUT_MS` a cikin `tests/integration/upstream-cli-smoke.int.test.ts`) — duba [CLI Integrations → Gagarumin smoke sweep](../guides/CLI-INTEGRATIONS.md).                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_BUILDING`                       | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/buildPhase.ts`                               | Siginar matakin gini (#10060): `scripts/build/build-next-isolated.mjs` ne ke saita shi zuwa `1` kuma kowane build worker da aka haifa yana gajinsa don DB layer ya mayar da no-op stub maimakon loda native better-sqlite3 addon (wanda ke dakatar da worker lokacin fita). Kada a taɓa saita shi ga ma'aikatar da ke gudana.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_NATIVE_DEP_CHECK`          | `0`                           | `scripts/check/check-native-deps.mjs`                 | Saita zuwa `1` don tsallake binciken prebuild na zaɓi na native-dependency don exotic vendored trees. Wannan bai sa dependency da ba a samu ba ta zama mai iya gina ba; yi amfani da shi kawai lokacin da aka samar da native dependencies daga waje.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_DATA_DIR`                       | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `open-sse/executors/promptql/threadSticky.ts`         | **Sunan madadi na fallback** na `DATA_DIR`, ana dubansa kawai lokacin da ba a saita `DATA_DIR` ba. Ana amfani da shi don nemo on-disk thread-sticky session cache na PromptQL executor (`<dir>/promptql-thread-sessions.json`); idan ba a saita ɗayan masu canjin ba, cache ɗin yana zama a cikin memory kawai (ba a adana shi a tsakanin restarts).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_PLUGINS_DIR`                    | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/plugins/scanner.ts`                          | Direktilin da **runtime plugin scanner** ke karantawa — kuma tushen da plugin manager ke sanyawa ciki — yana maye tsohon da aka samo daga home (#11827). Nuna shi zuwa bind-mounted plugin tree a cikin Docker/K8s maimakon motsa HOME kawai don canza hanyar scan (HOME yana sarrafa duk sauran halayen da suka dogara da home ma). Ba a saita ba = `~/.omniroute/plugins`, ko `/tmp/.omniroute/plugins` lokacin da process bai fitar da home gaba ɗaya ba — rashin gano cikin shiru wanda wannan mai canji ke cirewa. Ana rubuta direktilin da aka warware sau ɗaya a fara aiki a matsayin `scanner.dir_resolved` tare da input ɗin da ya yi nasara. Server-side kawai: CLI command plugins suna da nasu `OMNIROUTE_PLUGIN_PATH` (sashe 9). |
| `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY`                   | _(babu komai = an kashe)_     | `src/lib/db/encryption.ts`                            | Maɓallin AES don cikakken ɓoyewar bayanan SQLite yayin da yake a hali na ajiya. Ƙirƙira shi da `openssl rand -hex 32`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY_VERSION`           | `v1`                          | `scripts/build/bootstrap-env.mjs`, `electron/main.js` | Alamar sigar maɓallin ɓoyewa. Ƙara ta lokacin da ake yin mayar da maɓalli (key rotation) don tallafawa buɗe tsoffin ajiyayyen bayanai.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `DISABLE_SQLITE_AUTO_BACKUP`               | `false`                       | `src/lib/db/backup.ts`                                | Lokacin da ya zama `true`, yana tsallake ajiyayyen fayil ɗin SQLite na yau da kullum/kafin rubutu (ajiyewar/gogewar farashi na models.dev, rubutun saituna). Ajiyayyen hannu da na kafin dawowa suna ci gaba da aiki. **Ba** ya kashe hoton tsaro na dole na mai gudanar da ƙaura ko mai tsaron ƙaurawa babba don DB mai dorewa da yake nan akwai. An takaita ajiyayyen da ba na hannu ba zuwa mafi yawan sau ɗaya a kowane minti 60. **Settings → Storage** na Dashboard na iya kashe ajiyayyen kai tsaye na yau da kullum shi kaɗai.                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_SQLITE_SLOW_MS`                 | _(ba a saita ba/kashe)_       | `src/lib/db/adapters/slowDiagnostics.ts`              | Millisecond; ƙimomin da suka ƙasa da 1 ko waɗanda ba lambobi ba suna kashe; yana gargaɗi mafi yawan sau ɗaya a kowane sakan 10; layin log yana da direba, aiki, da elapsedMs kawai.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_CRYPT_KEY`                      | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/db/encryption.ts`                            | **Sunan tsoho na madaji** ga `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY`. Ana karɓarsa a matsayin madaji lokacin da babban canjin babu shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_BASE64`                 | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/db/encryption.ts`                            | **Sunan tsoho na madaji** (tsarin Base64) da ake karɓa a matsayin madaji. Ana fassara shi da kai tsaye kafin amfani.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_DB_HEALTHCHECK_INTERVAL_MS`     | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/db/core.ts`                                  | Canja tsakanin binciken lafiya na SQLite na lokaci-lokaci (ms). Lokacin da ba a saita ba, ana samun tsafo daga `NODE_ENV`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_WAL_TRUNCATE_INTERVAL_MS`       | _(an cire)_                   | `src/lib/db/walMaintenance.ts`                        | **An cire.** `wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE)` na kai tsaye na lokaci-lokaci na iya sanya taswirar wal-index ta raba ba ta aiki kuma ya rushe tsarin da SIGBUS (#13973), don haka mai tsarawa ba ya nan. Canjin bai da tasiri: ƙima mai kyau tana rubuta gargaɗin cirewa sau ɗaya kawai, yayin da `0` ko rashin saitawa ke shiru. Ana kula da WAL ta hanyar matakan PASSIVE (a ƙasa) kuma ana guntawa shi ta matakin rufewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_WAL_PASSIVE_INTERVAL_MS`        | `300000` (minti 5)            | `src/lib/db/walMaintenance.ts`                        | Canja tsakanin `wal_checkpoint(PASSIVE)` na akai-akai (ms). Yana riƙe da firam ɗin WAL da ke jira su kasance ƙanana domin matakan su ci gaba da kasancewa masu sauri kuma fayil ɗin WAL ya kasance a ƙarƙashin iyaka tsakanin guntawar rufewa. `0` yana kashewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_WAL_GUARD_MAX_MB`               | `256`                         | `src/lib/db/walMaintenance.ts`                        | Lokacin da matakin PASSIVE ya ga fayil ɗin WAL ya wuce wannan girman, gudanar da `wal_checkpoint(RESTART)` domin WAL ya fara sabo ba tare da sake rubuta wal-index da aka tsara ba. An cire matakan yanayin guntawa na kai tsaye (dubi jerin `OMNIROUTE_WAL_TRUNCATE_INTERVAL_MS`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_SELF_RESTART`          | `false`                       | `open-sse/utils/resourcePressure.ts`                  | Saita zuwa `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` don fita daga tsarin bayan an ci gaba da matsin albarkatu mai muni na tsawon `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_SELF_RESTART_AFTER_MS`, yana barin mai kula (systemd `Restart=always`, manufar sake kunna Docker) ya dawo da tsari tsaftace maimakon ci gaba da ba da 503s ba tare da iyaka ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_SELF_RESTART_AFTER_MS` | `120000` (minti 2)            | `open-sse/utils/resourcePressure.ts`                  | Tsawon lokacin da dole ne matsi mai muni ya dawwama kafin fitar sake kunna kai ta fara aiki.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_PSI_DISABLED`          | `false`                       | `open-sse/utils/resourcePressurePolicy.ts`            | Saita zuwa `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` don watsi da `/proc/pressure/memory` na duk mai masauki (`psi_some` / `psi_full`) lokacin da maƙwabci mai hayaniya ya haifar da 503s yayin da wannan tsarin yana da kyau (#13124). Masu tsaron Cgroup da V8 heap suna ci gaba da aiki. PSI kuma yana daina ƙidaya a matsayin hujjar matsa lamba na kernel ga `cgroup_high`: jimillar cgroup sama da `memory.high` sai ta zama mai muni kawai lokacin da `memory.events` `high`/`max` ya ƙaru. Ba tare da `memory.events` ba, ana riƙe da rabo da yau.                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_SQLJS_WASM_PATH`                | _(ganowa ta kai tsaye)_       | `src/lib/db/adapters/sqljsAdapter.ts`                 | Hanya a fili (cikakkiya ko dangantaka da cwd) zuwa `sql-wasm.wasm` lokacin amfani da mai daidaita madadin WASM na `sql.js`. Ana gano shi da kai tsaye ta hanyar dogaro na fakiti da tsare-tsaren da ake zato lokacin da ba a saita ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_BATCH_RETENTION_DAYS`           | `30`                          | `src/lib/db/cleanup.ts`                               | Kwanakin da tsarin tsabtacewar kai tsaye yake ajiye checkpoints, fayilolin input/output/error da aka nuna, da jere na aikin Batch API wanda ya kare (completed/failed/cancelled/expired) kafin a share su. Yana aiki ne kawai bayan an kunna `BATCH_AND_FILE_AUTO_CLEANUP_ENABLED`; yayi daidai da lokacin ajiyar sakamako na Batch API na OpenAI. Ba ya shafar hanyar `DELETE /api/v1/batches/delete-completed` wadda mai aiki yake kaddamarwa, wadda take ci gaba da zama ba tare da sharadi ba (babu tacewar shekaru) bisa tsari.                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `BATCH_AND_FILE_AUTO_CLEANUP_ENABLED`      | `false`                       | `src/lib/db/cleanup.ts`                               | Idan `true`, bari tsarin tsabtacewar kai tsaye ya share ayyukan Batch API da suka kare (da checkpoints ɗinsu) da suka wuce `OMNIROUTE_BATCH_RETENTION_DAYS`, kuma ya share abun cikin BLOB na fayilolin da aka loda da suka wuce `expires_at` nasu. Kashe a tuni: kowace shigarwa da ke akwai tana ci gaba da ajiye wannan bayanai kamar yadda yake har sai mai aiki ya zaɓi shiga. Har ila yau, feature flag ne da za a iya gyarawa a dashboard — duba `docs/reference/FEATURE_FLAGS.md` → Runtime.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_DB_HEALTHCHECK`            | `0`                           | `src/lib/db/core.ts`, `src/lib/db/healthCheck.ts`     | Saita zuwa `1` don tsallake duba lafiyar DB gaba ɗaya lokacin farawa. Yana da amfani ga ayyukan da ba su daɗewa da kuma gwaje-gwajen haɗawa (integration tests).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_FORCE_DB_HEALTHCHECK`           | `0`                           | `src/lib/db/core.ts`                                  | Saita zuwa `1` don tilasta kunna maimaitawar duba lafiyar DB, ko da a lokacin da ake tsallakewa a al'ada (misali, ayyukan da ba su daɗewa).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_POSTINSTALL`               | `0`                           | `scripts/postinstall.mjs`                             | Saita zuwa `1` don tsallake shirye-shiryen native-runtime a lokacin `npm install`. Yana da amfani a shigarwar CI/headless inda an riga an gina sqlite.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_MIGRATIONS_DIR`                 | _(ganowa ta atomatik)_        | `src/lib/db/migrationRunner.ts`                       | Maye gurbin kundayen da mai gudanar da migration yake duba. Yana da amfani lokacin aika migrations da aka haɗa a gine-ginen musamman (custom builds).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_EXTRA_MIGRATIONS_DIRS`          | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/db/migrationRunner/extraDirs.ts`             | Ƙarin kundayen migration a matsayin shigarwar `namespace=dir` da aka raba da alamar rabar hanyar dandamali (misali `ee=/opt/app/enterprise/db/migrations`). Ana rubuta fayilolin da aka samu a can a matsayin `<namespace>-<number>`, don haka rarrabawa wadda take aika migrations nata ba za ta taɓa cin karo da lambobin upstream ba. Shigarwar da ba daidai ba, namespace marar inganci ko kundayen da bai nan yana jefa kuskure a lokacin farawa maimakon tsallake schema a shiru.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_MAX_PENDING_MIGRATIONS`         | `50`                          | `src/lib/db/migrationRunner.ts`                       | Ƙofar kariya ta migrations masu yawa da ke jiran aiki (#3416). Farawa yana tsayawa idan migrations fiye da wannan adadin suna jiran aiki a kan DB da ke akwai (kariya daga shafe teburin bin diddiga). Tada shi don dawo da tsofaffin ajiyayye; saita zuwa `0` don kashe binciken.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_INSTALL_UPGRADE_WORKDIR`        | _(`<repo>/.install-upgrade`)_ | `scripts/check/check-install-upgrade.mjs`             | Kundayen aiki na ƙofar sakin sigar `check:install-upgrade`. Yana buƙatar kusan 12 GB (itacen shigarwa guda biyu ~3 GB tare da tarball), don haka bai kamata a gudanar da shi a kan ƙaramin tmpfs ba — a kan self-hosted runner, `/tmp` tmpfs ne na 12 GB da RAM ke tallafawa kuma ƙofar ta gusar da shi, yana yanke kunshin.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_SPEND_FLUSH_INTERVAL_MS`        | _(na tuni a cikin lambar)_    | `src/lib/spend/batchWriter.ts`                        | Tsawon lokacin zubawa (ms) na mai rubuta kashe kuɗi/cost a rukuni. Ƙananan ƙima suna rage haɗa rubutu; manyan ƙima suna rage rikicin DB.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_SPEND_MAX_BUFFER_SIZE`          | _(na tuni a cikin lambar)_    | `src/lib/spend/batchWriter.ts`                        | Matsakaicin shigarwar kashe kuɗi da aka ajiye a buffer kafin tilasta zubawa. Tada shi a sassaɓa masu babban QPS; rage shi lokacin da iyakancewar ƙwaƙwalwa muhimmanci ya fi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_FETCH_DEBUG`              | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`                        | Saita zuwa `"true"` don fitar da rajistan gyaran kuskure `[ProxyFetch]` a kan hanyar relay ta Vercel. Kashe a tuni don guje wa bayyana alamun jagora (routing hints).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_CORE_BINARY_PATH`         | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/proxySubscription/coreConfig/sync.ts`        | Cikakkiyar hanya ta binary na proxy core na gida (fayil mai suna `sing-box`) wanda yake duba tsarin core da aka samar kafin ya maye gurbin fayil ɗin da aka karɓa. Rashin saita shi yana rubuta `<path>.generated` kusa da fayil ɗin da aka karɓa kawai. Yanayin mai masauki kawai: ba a ajiye shi a rumbun bayanai ba kuma ba a karɓa shi ta hanyar management API ba, saboda ana aiwatar da binary.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_CORE_RELOAD_COMMAND`      | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/proxySubscription/coreConfig/reload.ts`      | Yadda proxy core na gida yake sake loda bayan maye gurbin tsarin da aka tabbatar: jerin muhawwara na JSON da ake gudanarwa ba tare da shell ba, ko daidai `external` idan mai kallo daga waje yake aiwatar da fayil ɗin. Ba a ajiye shi a rumbun bayanai ba kuma ba a bayyana shi ta API ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `PROXY_LOG_INCLUDE_IPS`                    | `false`                       | `src/lib/proxyLogger.ts`                              | Saita zuwa `"true"` ko `"1"` don haɗa IPs na client/egress da prefix na asusu a cikin layin process-log mai verbose na `[ProxyEgress]`. An bar shi a OFF tso don process log baya zubar da IPs ko prefix na asusu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_DEBUG`                          | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `bin/cli/commands/quota.mjs`                          | Saita zuwa `1` don buga bayanan binciken lokacin kowace buƙata (`[omniroute] GET <path> completed in Nms`) daga umarnin quota na CLI zuwa stderr.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_HEALTHCHECK_PATH`               | _(ta atomatik)_               | `scripts/dev/healthcheck.mjs`                         | Hanya da aka fayyace wadda binciken lafiyar container ke dubawa. Idan ba a saita ba, probe yana samunta daga `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`; saita ta yana komawa cikin deep monitoring endpoint.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_DEBUG_COMPLETION`               | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `bin/cli/commands/completion.mjs`                     | Saita zuwa kowace ƙima da ba fanko ba don fitar da bayanan binciken `[omniroute completion]` daga hanyoyin cache na shell-completion na CLI (karanta/sabunta/rubuta). A kashe tso — waɗancan caches suna gaza a shiru don cache da ya ɓace ko ya lalace baya taɓa karya tab-completion.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `BATCH_RETRY_DURATION_MS`                  | `86400000` (24h)              | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Mafi girman tagar sake gwaji don kowane abu na batch (ms). Abubuwan da suka wuce wannan tsawon lokaci ana alamta su a matsayin sun gaza.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `BATCH_BACKOFF_BASE_MS`                    | `5000`                        | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Jinkirin tushe (ms) don exponential backoff a kan sake gwadar abubuwan batch.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `BATCH_BACKOFF_MAX_MS`                     | `3600000` (1h)                | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Iyaka (ms) don exponential backoff tsakanin sake gwadar abubuwan batch.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `BATCH_MAX_CONCURRENT`                     | `1`                           | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Mafi girman adadin batches da ake sarrafawa a lokaci guda. Ɗaga shi don ƙara throughput; riƙe shi ƙasa don guje wa rate-limit storms.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |

> [!IMPORTANT]
> Kafin a canza wani database mai dawwama da yake akwai, mai gudanar da migration yana buga cikakken,
> content-addressed snapshot a ƙarƙashin `DATA_DIR/db_backups/`. Bugawar yana buƙatar filesystem
> wanda yake goyon bayan hard links na filesystem guda marar-rubutawa-bisa tare da file sync mai dawwama. Masu masaukin POSIX suma
> suna buƙatar directory sync; akan Windows, Node na iya ƙin directory handles, don haka OmniRoute yana yi wa
> fayil ɗin da aka buga flush kuma yana ɗaukar sync na directory-entry a matsayin best effort.
> Idan `DATA_DIR` da aka mount ba zai iya ba da waɗancan tabbacin ba, startup yana gaza a rufe kafin aiwatar da
> migration. Matsar da `DATA_DIR` zuwa wani volume mai waɗannan abubuwan farko; kada a yi amfani da
> `DISABLE_SQLITE_AUTO_BACKUP` don kauce wa tsaron migration.

### Yanayin

| Yanayin             | Kanfigareshan                                                                       |
| ------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Ci gaba na gida** | Bar duk tsoffin saitunan. DB yana a `~/.omniroute/omniroute.db`.                    |
| **Docker**          | `DATA_DIR=/data` + mount wani volume a `/data`.                                     |
| **Ɓoyayye a hutu**  | Saita `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY` + riƙe backups na maɓallin! Rashinsa = rasa bayanai. |
| **CI/Gwaji**        | `DATA_DIR=/tmp/omniroute-test` — na wucin gadi, ba a buƙatar ɓoyewa.                |

---

## 3. Hanyar Sadarwa & Tashoshi

| Variable                                    | Default                                             | Source File                                                              | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `PORT`                                      | `20128`                                             | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Babban tashar don duk Dashboard UI da wuraren ƙarshen API (yanayin tashar ɗaya).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`                       | _(fanko = asali)_                                   | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/docker/ensure-docker-base-path.mjs`          | Ƙaramin hanyar URL don hidimar OmniRoute a bayan reverse proxy (yana saita Next.js `basePath`; hanyoyin sauya shiga suna sane da basePath). Misali `/omniroute`. A cikin Docker ana saƙa ƙimar ne yayin `docker build` (`ARG OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`); hotunan asali da aka riga aka gina za su iya amfani da ƙimar lokacin aiki daban sau ɗaya a fara container kafin Next.js ya tashi. Saita `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL` zuwa asalin jama'a gami da wannan ƙaramin hanya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`           | _(fanko = asali)_                                   | `src/shared/hooks/useDisplayBaseUrl.ts`                                  | Madubin da burauzar na gani na `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`, wanda aka haɗa a lokacin gina domin nuni na ƙarshen dashboard ya nuna `https://host/omniroute/v1` maimakon `https://host/v1`. Yana koma kan `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH` idan ba a saita ba. Sake gina bayan canjawa (Next `basePath` na lokacin gina ne).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `DASHBOARD_ALLOW_EMBED`                     | _(ba a saita ba = ba za a iya saka shi a frame ba)_ | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/build/dashboardEmbed.mjs`                    | Zaɓin shigar da iframe na shafukan HTML. Idan ba a saita ba, kowace hanya tana fitowa da `frame-ancestors 'none'` + `X-Frame-Options: DENY`. Saita zuwa `vscode` don hidimar shafukan (dashboard, shiga, takardun, farko) tare da `frame-ancestors 'self' vscode-webview:` kuma babu `X-Frame-Options`, domin VS Code Simple Browser ya iya nuna su (yanayin `dashboardOpen: "editor"` na OmniCopilot). Fuskar API (`/api`, `/v1`, `/v1beta`, `/a2a`, `/healthz`, laƙabobi na matakin asali) tana riƙe da kananan takardun masu tsauri ko yaya. Sai kawai `vscode` ake gane — `1`/`true` ba za su kunna shi ba. Lokacin gina: sake gina bayan canjawa (`docker build --build-arg DASHBOARD_ALLOW_EMBED=vscode` don hotuna; saita shi a kan shigarwa da aka riga aka gina ba ta da tasiri).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `API_PORT`                                  | _(ba a saita ba)_                                   | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Idan an saita, yana hidimar `/v1/*` proxy API a wannan tashar daban.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `API_HOST`                                  | `0.0.0.0`                                           | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Adireshin ɗaure don tashar API.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `DASHBOARD_PORT`                            | _(ba a saita ba)_                                   | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Idan an saita, yana hidimar Dashboard UI a wannan tashar daban.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNI_MAX_CONCURRENT_CONNECTIONS`           | `0` _(an kashe)_                                    | `src/sse/utils/backpressure.ts`                                          | Yana sa iyaka kan haɗin tattaunawa masu gudana a lokaci ɗaya; buƙatun da suka wuce iyaka suna samun `503` tare da `Retry-After`. Cikakkiyar lamba mai kyau tana kunna gadi; idan ba a saita ba/`0` yana kashe shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_INSTANCE_ID`                     | _(ba a saita ba)_                                   | `src/shared/resilience/peerRouting.ts`                                   | ID kafaffen kuma na musamman don wannan ƙofa idan ana sarƙaƙa misalan OmniRoute tare. Yana ba da damar binciken madauki na abokan aiki masu shigowa. Haruffan da ake yarda da su: haruffa, lambobi, `.`, `_`, `:`, da `-`; mafi yawa haruffa 64.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_PEER_URLS`                       | _(ba a saita ba)_                                   | `src/shared/resilience/peerRouting.ts`, `open-sse/executors/base.ts`     | URLs na tushen OmniRoute da aka raba da waƙafi waɗanda za su iya karɓar `X-OmniRoute-Peer-Trace`. URLs na upstream da aka saka a jerin ƙyalewa kawai ke karɓar metadata na peer; sauran masu samarwa ba a taɓa su ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_PEER_MAX_HOPS`                   | `4`                                                 | `src/shared/resilience/peerRouting.ts`                                   | Matsakaicin adadin instances na OmniRoute da aka ziyarta a baya da ake karɓa a kan buƙata mai sarƙaƙƙiya (`1`-`32`). Idan an maimaita instance ko kasafin ya ƙare, ana mayar da HTTP `508 Loop Detected`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `PROD_DASHBOARD_PORT`                       | `20130`                                             | `docker-compose.prod.yml`                                                | Tashar da aka wallafa daga gefen host don Dashboard a yanayin production na Docker.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `PROD_API_PORT`                             | `20131`                                             | `docker-compose.prod.yml`                                                | Tashar da aka wallafa daga gefen host don API a yanayin production na Docker.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

| `LIVE_WS_PORT`                              | `20129`                                             | `src/server/ws/liveServer.ts`                                            | Tashar don uwar garken sa ido kai tsaye na WebSocket na ainihin lokaci.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |

Saita zuwa `0.0.0.0` don fallasa a kan LAN (haka ma saita `LIVE_WS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS`). Haɗin gwiwa suna tabbatarwa da kukin zama na dashboard ko maɓallin API na Bearer wanda yake riƙe da ikon gudanarwa (`manage`/`admin`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `LIVE_WS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS`                   | _(ba a saita ba)_                                   | `src/server/ws/liveServer.ts`                                            | Ƙarin origins da aka raba da waƙafi waɗanda aka ƙyale su buɗe WebSocket kai tsaye. An riga an ƙyale origins na loopback dashboard tuni ta tsoho.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `LIVE_WS_ALLOWED_HOSTS`                     | _(ba a saita ba)_                                   | `src/server/ws/liveServerAllowList.ts`                                   | Ƙarin sunayen host da aka raba da waƙafi waɗanda aka ƙyale don origins na WebSocket kai tsaye. Sabanin da `LIVE_WS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS` (cikakkun URLs na origin), yana daidaita ɓangaren host kawai — mai amfani don saitunan LAN/Tailscale.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_LIVE_WS_PUBLIC_URL`            | _(ba a saita ba)_                                   | `src/hooks/useLiveDashboard.ts`                                          | URL na jama'a don WebSocket na dashboard kai tsaye (gefen browser). Saita shi lokacin da ake sanya uwar garken WS a gaban reverse proxy ko Cloudflare Tunnel (misali `wss://ws.my-ai.com/live-ws`); browser yana haɗawa can maimakon `ws://hostname:20132`. Ana kuma amfani da ɓangaren pathname a matsayin hanyar upgrade ta WebSocket (tsoho: `/live-ws`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_ENABLE_LIVE_WS`                  | `true`                                              | `src/server/ws/liveServer.ts` da `scripts/start-ws-server.mjs`           | Saita zuwa `0` ko `false` don kashe uwar garken WebSocket na ainihin lokaci (an kunna ta tsoho, an ɗaure zuwa loopback). Canjin CI/harness wanda ke kashe rubutun taimako na WebSocket kai tsaye mai zaman kansa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `RELAY_IP_PER_MINUTE`                       | `30`                                                | `src/app/api/v1/relay/chat/completions/route.ts`                         | Iyakar yawan relay ga kowane (token, IP), buƙatu/minti.
