A cikin ƙwaƙwalwa, ga kowace instance. `0` ko lamba mara kyau tana kashe ƙofar ƙimar IP (iyakar DB ta kowane token har yanzu tana aiki).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `NODE_ENV`                                  | `production`                                        | Next.js core                                                             | Yana sarrafa zurfin logging, caching, bayyanar cikakkun bayanan kuskure, da haɓaka ayyukan Next.js.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_USE_TURBOPACK`                   | `1` (Turbopack — tsoho na lambar)                   | `package.json` / Next.js 16                                              | Turbopack shi ne bundler na tsoho don `npm run dev` da `npm run build` (gini mai sauri 2-3×, an aunana). Saita zuwa `0` don komawa kan webpack akan Windows, idan an fuskanci rashin dacewa na native binding / bundler-compat, **ko akan na'urori masu ƙarancin RAM** — an san a upstream cewa Turbopack production builds akan wannan jerin sigogin Next.js (16.2.x) suna kai kololuwa mafi tsawo a cikin ƙwaƙwalwar ajiya fiye da webpack akan manyan module graphs (gyaran memory-eviction na Turbopack na Next 16.3 bai cika tabbatacce ba tukuna); webpack fallback yana kai kololuwa mafi ƙasƙanci. Duba #6409. **Ginin Docker image yana da tsoho `0` (webpack)**: `Dockerfile` yana bayyana `ARG OMNIROUTE_USE_TURBOPACK=0` don a fili `docker build .` (Railway da sauran masu masauki na one-click, masu self-hosting na wucin gadi) baya samun OOM-kill akan mai gina da ƙwaƙwalwar ajiya da aka iyakance; wuce `--build-arg OMNIROUTE_USE_TURBOPACK=1` don gina hoton da Turbopack. Duba `docs/guides/DOCKER_GUIDE.md` → Albarkatun lokacin gini. |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_DB_HEALTHCHECK`             | _(ba a saita ba)_                                   | `src/lib/db/core.ts` / `src/lib/db/healthCheck.ts`                       | Saita zuwa `1` don tsallake binciken lafiyar mutunci na SQLite a lokacin farawa. Yana da amfani don saurin farawa akan manyan bayanai.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `NOTIFY_SOCKET`                             | _(ba a saita ba)_                                   | systemd (yarjejeniyar sd_notify)                                         | systemd ne ke saita shi lokacin da tsari yana gudana ƙarƙashin rukunin sabis tare da haɗin sd_notify; OmniRoute yana karanta shi (duba `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_SD_NOTIFY`) don aika sanarwar READY/WATCHDOG. Mai amfani ba ya saita shi ko ta kaɗan.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_SD_NOTIFY`               | _(ba a saita ba)_                                   | `scripts/dev/systemd-notify.mjs`                                         | Saita zuwa `1` don kashe systemd sd_notify (Type=notify / WatchdogSec=) ko da yana gudana ƙarƙashin rukunin systemd. Mai sanarwar ba ya aiki a waje da systemd ko yaya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CREDENTIAL_HEALTH_CHECK_INTERVAL`          | `3600000`                                           | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` / `src/lib/credentialHealth/scheduler.ts` | Tazara (ms) don mai tsara binciken lafiyar takardun shaida na bango. Mafi ƙanƙanta: 10000 (10s).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CREDENTIAL_HEALTH_CACHE_TTL`               | `300000`                                            | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` / `src/lib/credentialHealth/cache.ts`     | TTL (ms) don matsayin lafiyar takardun shaida da aka ajiye a cache.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_CREDENTIAL_HEALTH_CHECK` | `false`                                             | `src/lib/credentialHealth/scheduler.ts`                                  | Saita zuwa `1` ko `true` don kashe gwajin lokaci-lokaci na haɗin masu samarwa na bango. An keɓance masu samar da bincike (SEARCH_VALIDATOR_CONFIGS a cikin `src/lib/providers/validation/searchProviders.ts`, misali `tavily-search`) koyaushe daga zagayowar — "tantancewar" su ainihin tambaya ce ta upstream da ake biya, saboda haka ba a taɓa yin binciken lafiyar su a kan mai ƙidaya lokaci (#9970).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `DEEP_HEALTH_CHECK_ENABLED`                 | `0`                                                 | `src/app/api/monitoring/health/route.ts`                                 | Saita zuwa `1` don ba masu kira da aka tabbatar damar neman `/api/monitoring/health?deep=1`, wanda ke samfurin saman completions sau ɗaya a kowane TTL. Masu kira marasa suna ba sa taɓa haifar da binciken.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_EVENT_LOOP_STALL_THRESHOLD_MS`   | `5000`                                              | `src/lib/healthzLag.ts`                                                  | Ƙofar dakatarwa (ms) don mai rikodin event-loop na farawa: ɗaskarewa mafi tsawo fiye da wannan tana rubuta layi ɗaya `event loop stalled` a lokacin ci gaba tare da ƙidayar ƙwaƙwalwar ajiya da in-flight, kuma tana ƙara ƙidayar/mafi girma na `eventLoopStall` akan `/api/monitoring/health`. Saita zuwa `0` don kashewa; ƙimar da ba a iya karantawa ko mai negat tana komawa `5000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `HOST`                                      | `0.0.0.0`                                           | `scripts/dev/run-next.mjs`                                               | Adireshin ɗaurewa don sabar dev/start na Next.js. Yana ƙetare tsoho `0.0.0.0` idan an saita shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `HOSTNAME`                                  | `127.0.0.1`                                         | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`                                    | Adireshin ɗaurewa da mai gudanar da Playwright yake amfani da shi lokacin ƙaddamar da Next.js. Yana komawa `127.0.0.1` a matsayin tsoho don gwaje-gwaje masu warewa. **Kada a yi amfani da shi don `omniroute serve`** — a yi amfani da `OMNIROUTE_SERVER_HOST` maimakon haka (POSIX shells suna saita `HOSTNAME` kai tsaye zuwa sunan na'ura; `.env` ba zai iya maye gurbata shi ba).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_SERVER_HOST`                     | `0.0.0.0`                                           | `bin/cli/commands/serve.mjs`                                             | Adireshin ɗaurewa don `omniroute serve`. Yana guje wa karo da canjin `HOSTNAME` na POSIX shell (bash/zsh koyaushe suna saita shi zuwa sunan na'ura). Yana komawa zuwa `0.0.0.0` idan ba a saita ba. (#6194)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |

### Yanayin Port

```
┌───────────────────────────── Port Guda (tsoho) ─────────────────────────────┐
│  PORT=20128                                                                 │
│  → Dashboard: http://localhost:20128                                        │
│  → API:       http://localhost:20128/v1/chat/completions                    │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

┌─────────────────────────────── Rababbun Port ───────────────────────────────┐
│  DASHBOARD_PORT=20128                                                       │
│  API_PORT=20129                                                             │
│  API_HOST=0.0.0.0                                                           │
│  → Dashboard: http://localhost:20128                                        │
│  → API:       http://0.0.0.0:20129/v1/chat/completions                      │
│  Amfani: Bayyana API ga LAN yayin ake takaita Dashboard zuwa localhost.     │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

┌───────────────────────────── Docker Production ─────────────────────────────┐
│  PROD_DASHBOARD_PORT=443   PROD_API_PORT=8443                               │
│  → Daidaita ports na container zuwa na host a docker-compose.prod.yml.      │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

---

## 4. Tsaro & Tantancewa

| Variable                                    | Default                                                                                                   | Source File                                              | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| ------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `MACHINE_ID_SALT`                           | `endpoint-proxy-salt`                                                                                     | `src/lib/auth`                                           | Gishiri da aka haɗa da abubuwan gano na'ura domin ɗaukar sawun yatsa na na'ura (fingerprinting). Canza shi a kowace tura domin keɓancewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLI_SALT`                        | _(ba a saita ba = gishiri na bazuwar ga kowace shigarwa da aka adana a `<DATA_DIR>/cli-token-salt.json`)_ | `src/lib/machineToken.ts`                                | Gishirin HMAC don fitar da token ɗin tantancewar CLI na gida. Saita wannan ƙima yana juya duk token ɗin CLI a kan na'urar kuma koyaushe yana da fifiko a kan gishirin da aka adana. Duba `docs/security/CLI_TOKEN.md`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE`                        | `false`                                                                                                   | `src/lib/auth`                                           | Yana sa tutar `Secure` a kan kukis ɗin zama. **Dole ne ya kasance `true`** idan ana gudanar da shi a bayan HTTPS.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `REQUIRE_API_KEY`                           | `false`                                                                                                   | middleware ɗin API                                       | Idan `true`, duk buƙatun wakilin `/v1/*` dole ne su ƙunshi ingantaccen maɓallin API. **Wannan tuta ba ta kulle `GET /v1/models` ba**, wanda maimakon haka yana bin yanayin shigarwar dashboard (`requireAuthForModels`) — don haka `401` daga `/v1/models` BA ya nufin an kare inference ba. Duba `docs/security/INFERENCE_AUTH_POSTURE.md` (#13695).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `ALLOW_API_KEY_REVEAL`                      | `false`                                                                                                   | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts`         | Yana ba da damar bayyana cikakkun ƙimomin maɓallin API a cikin UI na Dashboard. Ana iya daidaita shi daga Feature Flags na Dashboard; haɗarin tsaro a kan misalan da ake raba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `NO_LOG_API_KEY_IDS`                        | _(fanko)_                                                                                                 | `src/lib/compliance/index.ts`                            | ID ɗin maɓallan API da aka raba da waƙafi waɗanda ke wucewa kan rajistar buƙatu (bin dokar GDPR).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `DEFAULT_RATE_LIMIT_PER_DAY`                | _(ba a saita ba = babu iyaka)_                                                                            | `src/shared/utils/apiKeyPolicy.ts`                       | Kasafin buƙatu na kowace rana na madadin da ake amfani da shi ga maɓallan API waɗanda ginshiƙin `rate_limits` ɗinsu ya kasance null. Ba a saita ba ko fanko: babu iyaka ta ɓoye (#2289, #11017). `0` daidai yake (babu iyaka). Cikakkiyar lamba mai tabbatacce N tana kunna N/rana, 5N/mako, 20N/wata. Ƙimomin da ba su da tsari kuma ba fanko ba suna komawa zuwa tagogin gado na 1000/rana, 5000/mako, 20000/wata.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `MAX_BODY_SIZE_BYTES`                       | `10485760` (10 MB)                                                                                        | `src/shared/middleware/bodySizeGuard.ts`                 | Mafi girman jikin buƙata da aka yarda. Yana ƙin kaya da suka wuce wannan iyaka.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_LARGE_BODY_BYTES`           | `262144` (256 KB)                                                                                         | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Haƙiƙanin jikin buƙatu waɗanda suka kai ko suka wuce wannan ƙofa suna ɗaukar lasisin shigarwa mai nauyi na atomatik wanda ke cikin tsari (process) kafin a yi JSON parsing (hanyar BYTE, ciki har da `POST /v1/responses`). Yana daidai da hanyar kubutarwa mai barin sararin lafiya ta [#10437](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/OmniRoute/pull/10437) kamar na waɗanda suka fi nauyi a tsari; har yanzu ana iyakance shi da `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES` / [#10110](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/OmniRoute/issues/10110) domin [#7849](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/OmniRoute/issues/7849) kada ya dawo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_HARD_MAX_BODY_BYTES`        | `52428800` (50 MB)                                                                                        | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Iyaka mai tsauri na hanyar chat da ake aiwatarwa a kan bytes da aka karanta yayin shigarwa mai iyaka, ciki har da buƙatu waɗanda `Content-Length` ɗinsu ya ɓace, ba shi da inganci, ko na ƙarya; abin da ya wuce yana karɓar `413`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_HEAVY_IN_FLIGHT`        | _(ba a saita ba — babu iyakar ƙidayar buƙatu)_                                                            | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | **#503-fanout:** wannan tsohon iyakar ƘIDAYAR buƙatu yanzu yana aiki ne kawai idan an saita shi a fili. Idan aka bar shi ba a saita ba (na asali), a maimakon haka ana sarrafa shigar da chat mai nauyi ta `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES` — kasafin BYTE da ake samu ta atomatik wanda aka ƙaddara daga iyakar ƙwaƙwalwar ajiya ta gaske ta process ɗin a **process guda** (V8 heap guda). `/v1/responses` biyu masu ratsawa na kusan 750k-token suna soke heaps na kusan 12 Gi (#7849) — gargaɗin **kasafin ƙwaƙwalwar ajiya** ne, ba iyakar samfur mai tsauri ta 2 ba. Process mai lafiya (heap ƙasa da rabin shed) YANA IYA karɓar ƙarin `/v1/responses` masu tsawo a lokaci guda ta `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_HEALTHY_HEADROOM`. Goma-goma na masu amfani na SSE masu tsawo (40–50) shi ne heap + `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES` / #10110, ba "max 2" ba. Ɗaga wannan a makare don "yi amfani da host" yana sake kawo #7849. Ninka **heaps** da **`DATA_DIR`s N masu zaman kansu** (#11024); kar a taɓa `replicas>1` a kan fayil ɗin SQLite guda.                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES`         | _(an samu ta atomatik)_                                                                                   | `src/shared/middleware/admissionBudget.ts`               | **#503-fanout:** override don kasafin byte na shigarwa da ake samu ta atomatik (25% na iyakar ƙwaƙwalwar ajiya V8/cgroup mafi tsauri da aka raba da ƙaruwa ta wucin gadi ta 8x). Ana ƙuntata ƙimomin da aka samu da waɗanda aka bayyana a fili zuwa 8 MiB–2 GiB. Jiki mafi girma fiye da kasafin da ke aiki yana gaza nan da nan da `413 body_exceeds_budget`; rikici tsakanin jikuna da za a iya bauta wa kaɗai-kaɗai yana ci gaba da zama `503` mai iya sake gwadawa. 40–50 na masu amfani na SSE masu tsawo a lokaci guda shi ne wannan kasafin + heap, ba "max 2" mai tsauri ba. Karanta `chatAdmission.maxInflightBytes` / `budgetSource` / `pressureSeverity` a `/api/monitoring/health` kafin yin daidaitawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_HEAP_SHED_RATIO`  | `0.75`                                                                                                    | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Rabin shed na matsin heap (`heapUsed / heap_size_limit`) don shigar da nauyi BYTE da STRUCTURE (#10183, #10268, #10437). Ana jefa buƙata mai nauyi ta lokaci guda da ta wuce `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_HEAVY_IN_FLIGHT` kawai da `503` mai iya sake gwadawa idan heap HAR YILA yana kan ko sama da wannan rabo; a kan heap mai lafiya ana karɓar ta ta hanyar ƙarfin ƙari na lafiya a maimakon haka.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_HEALTHY_HEADROOM` | `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_HEAVY_IN_FLIGHT` (na asali `1`)                                                       | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Ƙarfin ƙari mai iyaka don hanyar sauri ta heap mai lafiya (#10437) a **duka** STRUCTURE da BYTE (`admitChatRequest`, ciki har da jikuna ≥ `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_LARGE_BODY_BYTES`). Ba tare da wannan iyaka ba, kowace buƙata mai damuwa amma mai heap mai lafiya ta wuce shigarwa ba tare da wata sama ba. Da zarar wannan adadin leases na lokaci guda suna aiki ta hanyar heap mai lafiya, ƙarin buƙatu masu damuwa suna faɗowa zuwa WANNAN hanyar jira mai iyaka/shed da ake amfani da ita ƙarƙashin matsin heap na gaske. `0` yana kashe wucewa gaba ɗaya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_HEAVY_MESSAGE_COUNT`        | `200`                                                                                                     | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Ƙidayar saƙo da ke rarraba buƙatar chat a matsayin mai nauyi ko da lokacin da jikinta yana ƙasa da ƙofar byte.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_HEAVY_TOOL_COUNT`           | `64`                                                                                                      | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Ƙidayar kayan aiki da ke rarraba buƙatar chat a matsayin mai nauyi ko da lokacin da jikinta yana ƙasa da ƙofar byte.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_HEAVY_ESTIMATED_TOKENS`     | `32000`                                                                                                   | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Ƙididdigar token mai hankali daga girman string da ke rarraba buƙata a matsayin mai nauyi; wannan wakilin kuɗin shigarwa ne, ba tokenization na lissafin kuɗi na mai samarwa ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_HARD_MAX_MESSAGES`          | `0` (an kashe)                                                                                            | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Iyakar tarihin chat ta zaɓi da ake shiga da niyya. An kashe ta ta asali: ƙidayar saƙo manufar tura aiki ce, ba dabi'a ta duniya ta buƙata ba, kuma iyakancewa a nan yana ƙin tattaunawa da `413` na ƙarshe kafin layin matsewa ya sanya su abubuwan da za a iya bautawa. Ana iyakance ci gaban heap da `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_HEAVY_IN_FLIGHT` da shed na matsin heap. Saita ƙima mai kyau a kan tura ayyuka masu ƙuntataccen ƙwaƙwalwar ajiya waɗanda ke buƙatar sama mai tsauri; sa'an nan abin da ya wuce yana karɓar `413` na tsari mai buƙatar compact-required.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_MAX_NONSTREAMING_RESPONSE_BYTES` | `67108864` (64 MB)                                                                                        | `open-sse/handlers/chatCore/nonStreamingResponseBody.ts` | Iyaka mai tsauri don amsar upstream mara tsinkaya da aka adana gaba ɗaya a ƙwaƙwalwar ajiya. Bayan wannan ana soke mai karatu na upstream kuma buƙatar tana gaza da sauri maimakon haɓaka string mara iyaka har sai an gama heap.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_FORWARDING_HEADER_BUDGET_BYTES`  | `768`                                                                                                     | `open-sse/handlers/chatCore/responseHeaders.ts`          | Mafi yawan bytes na waya da ake turawa daga headers na amsar upstream. Idan an wuce kasafin, ana jefar da headers masu ƙarancin fifiko (misali, `x-codex-*` na al'ada, `x-oai-request-id`) don su kasance cikin iyakokin header na reverse-proxy na yau da kullum. Saita shi mafi girma don turawa ƙarin metadata na upstream a farashin ƙaruwar girman header na amsa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `CORS_ORIGIN`                               | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                                                         | `src/server/cors/origins.ts`                             | Jerin ƙyalewar CORS na tushe ɗaya na dā. Fi son `CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS` don sababbin turaɗe. CORS ga abokan ciniki na API na browser na cross-origin kawai ne; rubuce-rubucen dashboard da aka tantance suna amfani da buƙatun same-origin tare da kariyar CSRF da aka ɗaure da session maimakon haka.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS`                      | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                                                         | `src/server/cors/origins.ts`                             | Jerin ƙyalewar CORS da aka raba da waƙafi. Ba a aika wildcard sai idan an saita `CORS_ALLOW_ALL=true` a fili.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CORS_ALLOW_ALL`                            | `false`                                                                                                   | `src/server/cors/origins.ts`                             | Ƙofar tsira ta gaggawa ta development kawai don mayar da martani ga kowane `Origin` na browser. Kada a kunna shi a turaɗe na tarayya ko na production.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OUTBOUND_SSRF_GUARD_ENABLED`               | `true`                                                                                                    | `src/shared/network/outboundUrlGuardPolicy.ts`           | Laƙabi na dā. Ana karanta toggle na dashboard nata (wani override na DB) kafin wannan ƙimar muhalli; `false`, `0`, `no`, ko `off` a ko ɗayansu yana kashe binciken host yadda `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_PRIVATE_PROVIDER_URLS=true` yake yi, ko da kuwa an adana toggle na dashboard na wannan tuta a kashe; sauran ƙimomin suna barin gadi ga tutocin biyu da ke ƙasa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_PRIVATE_PROVIDER_URLS`     | `false`                                                                                                   | `src/shared/network/outboundUrlGuardPolicy.ts`           | Yana kashe binciken host na gadin URL na fita, gami da toshewar cloud-metadata (duk 169.254.0.0/16, metadata.google.internal), a kan hanyoyin provider da ke bin yanayin gadi: tantance URL, binciken samfura, base URLs na provider-node (ciki har da nodes na rerank na nesa), da gwajin proxy-fallback, da sauran hanyoyin fita masu alaƙa da provider kamar kawo hoto/media daga nesa. Hakanan yana ƙyale manufofin webhook masu zaman kansu, sai ƙarshen cloud-metadata/link-local, waɗanda ke ci gaba da zama a toshe ko da wannan tuta tana kunne. Buƙatun chat da aka aika ta `BaseExecutor.execute()` suna ci gaba da binciken cloud-metadata nasu. Providers na gida suna aiki da tsafo: `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_LOCAL_PROVIDER_URLS` (tsoho `true`) ya riga ya ƙyale URLs na gida da LAN a kan hanyoyin tantancewa, bincike, da provider-node; gwajin proxy-fallback da manufofin webhook masu zaman kansu suna tuntuɓar wannan tuta kawai kuma suna toshe hosts na gida/LAN yayin da take a kashe. Yana rinjayar `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_LOCAL_PROVIDER_URLS=false`. Ƙimar da aka adana daga toggle na dashboard tana da fifiko a kan wannan canji.                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_LOCAL_PROVIDER_URLS`       | `true`                                                                                                    | `src/shared/network/outboundUrlGuardPolicy.ts`           | Ƙyale URLs na provider a kan adiresoshin gida/masu zaman kansu (127.0.0.1, localhost, LAN, kewayon masu zaman kansu) don tantance provider, binciken samfura, chat, da sauran hanyoyin fita masu alaƙa da provider da yanayin gadi ke kula da su. **Tsoho `true`** (gida-da-farko): gadin sai ya toshe ƙarshen cloud-metadata — duk 169.254.0.0/16 tare da sanannun sunayen host na metadata (metadata.google.internal, metadata.goog, Alibaba/Azure/Oracle IMDS, fd00:ec2::254). Saita `false` don yanayin jama'a-kawai, wanda ke toshe hosts masu zaman kansu da loopback kuma. Gadin yana bincika sunan host ko IP literal kamar yadda aka rubuta shi, kafin kowane binciken DNS; a yanayin jama'a-kawai, wrapper na safe-fetch yana ƙara warware sunan host kuma yana ƙin kowace amsa mai zaman kanta. `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_PRIVATE_PROVIDER_URLS=true` yana kashe waɗannan binciken a kan hanyoyin da jerin nasa ya lissafa. Providers na gida da aka gina ciki, kamar LM Studio, Ollama, vLLM, Llamafile, da Triton, suna tsallake duk binciken host don tantance maɓalli da chat. Ƙimar da aka adana daga toggle na dashboard tana da fifiko a kan wannan canji. (#5066) |
| `AUDIO_REMOTE_PROVIDER_NODES`               | `false`                                                                                                   | `src/app/api/v1/_shared/audioProviderNodes.ts`           | Bari hanyoyin `/v1/audio/*` (juyin sauti zuwa rubutu, samar da magana, fassarori) su yi amfani da provider node mai dacewa da OpenAI da aka masauki a wajen localhost. A kashe ne tsoho — tura sauti zuwa host na nesa yana canza ainihin fita (egress) kuma dole ne ya zama ƙudurin mai gudanarwa a fili. Nodes na loopback/masu zaman kansu (localhost, 127.0.0.1, 172.16-31.x) ana ƙyale su koyaushe kuma ba su da tasiri. (#3963)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `RERANK_REMOTE_PROVIDER_NODES`              | `false`                                                                                                   | `src/app/api/v1/_shared/rerankProviderNodes.ts`          | Bari `POST /v1/rerank` (da matakin rerank na loopback na injin ƙwaƙwalwa) su yi amfani da provider node mai dacewa da OpenAI da aka masauki a wajen localhost — kwamfuta ta LAN ko abokin Tailscale da ke gudanar da TEI, Infinity, vLLM, da dai sauransu. A kashe ne tsoho — tura zuwa host na nesa yana canza ainihin fita kuma dole ne ya zama ƙudurin mai gudanarwa a fili. Nodes na loopback (localhost, 127.0.0.1, 172.16-31.x) ana ƙyale su koyaushe kuma ba su da tasiri. Base URL na node na nesa dole ne kuma ya wuce manuɗar URL na fita ta provider (`OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_LOCAL_PROVIDER_URLS` / `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_PRIVATE_PROVIDER_URLS`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOCAL_PROVIDER_NODE_HOSTS`       | _(empty)_                                                                                                 | `src/shared/network/localNodeHosts.ts`                   | Sunayen host (wanda aka raba da waƙafi ko sararin fari) waɗanda tsarin tura buƙatu na provider-node ke ɗauka a matsayin na cikin gida — yawanci sunan sabis na Docker/Compose na reranker, TEI ko sidecar na audio a kan hanyar sadarwa ta Docker ta OmniRoute (`http://reranker:8080/v1`), domin node-in kada ya buƙaci adireshin IP na kwantena da aka buga a tsaye. Hosts da aka jera suna cancanta don `/v1/rerank`, `/v1/audio/*` da duba lafiyar gida ba tare da kunna `RERANK_REMOTE_PROVIDER_NODES` / `AUDIO_REMOTE_PROVIDER_NODES` ga kowane node mai nisa ba, kuma ana kiran su kai tsaye — ba ta `HTTP(S)_PROXY` ko proxy wanda aka kafa ga haɗin ba. Suna ci gaba da aika maɓallin API na haɗin. Ana karɓar sunayen maki ɗaya kawai da sunayen `.internal`/`.local`; ana watsi da FQDN na jama'a, adreshin IP kai tsaye da sunayen cloud-metadata tare da gargaɗi. (#14635)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_OIDC_DISABLE_PASSWORD_LOGIN`     | `false`                                                                                                   | `src/app/api/auth/login/route.ts`                        | Lokacin da aka kunna OIDC, kashe shiga da kalmar sirri domin masu amfani su iya tantancewa kawai ta hanyar OIDC Single Sign-On. Ana kuma karɓar takaitaccen sunan `OIDC_DISABLE_PASSWORD_LOGIN`; Feature Flag na Dashboard mai wannan maɓalli shine ke da fifiko. (#10889)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OIDC_DISABLE_PASSWORD_LOGIN`               | `false`                                                                                                   | `src/app/api/auth/login/route.ts`                        | Takaitaccen sunan alias na `OMNIROUTE_OIDC_DISABLE_PASSWORD_LOGIN` (#10889).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |

### Jerin Duba Ƙarfafa Tsaro

```bash
# Mafi ƙarancin tsaro na samarwa (production):
AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE=true        # Yana buƙatar HTTPS
REQUIRE_API_KEY=true           # Tantance duk kiran proxy
ALLOW_API_KEY_REVEAL=false     # Kada a taɓa bayyana maɓallai a UI
CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS=https://your.domain.com
MAX_BODY_SIZE_BYTES=5242880    # Iyakar 5 MB
```

---

## 5. Tsabtace Bayanai Masu Shigowa & Kare PII

OmniRoute yana samar da kariya mai matakai biyu: binciken yunƙurin kutse a ɓangaren buƙata da cire PII a ɓangaren martani.

> **⚠️ Iyakoki:** Waɗannan matakan kariya bincike ne na _hasashe gwargwadon ƙoƙari_, ba cikakken bangon kariya daga kutse ta hanyar prompt ko tsarin PII DLP ba. Za su iya haifar da sakamako na ƙarya (kamar yi wa prompts na persona/RPG marasa lahani alama) da kuma kasa gano barazana (kamar leetspeak, tazara, da tsarukan da ba na Turanci ba). Su kaɗai ba su isa wajen bin ƙa'idodi ba. Daidaita modes ɗin kuma gwada su da zirga-zirgarku kafin ku dogara da su.

### Ɓangaren Buƙata: Kariyar Kutse ta Prompt

| Maɓalli                           | Tsoho             | Fayil ɗin Tushe                          | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| --------------------------------- | ----------------- | ---------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `INPUT_SANITIZER_ENABLED`         | `true`            | `src/middleware/promptInjectionGuard.ts` | Kunna binciken saƙonni masu shigowa domin gano tsarukan kutse ta prompt.                                                                                                                           |
| `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE`            | `warn`            | `src/middleware/promptInjectionGuard.ts` | Manufofin kutse: `warn` = rubuta log kawai, `block` = ƙin buƙatar tare da 400. Tsohon `redact` **ba ya** cire rubutun kutse; yi amfani da `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED` domin sake rubuta PII na buƙata. |
| `INJECTION_GUARD_MODE`            | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `src/middleware/promptInjectionGuard.ts` | Tsohon madadin suna na `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE` — halayya iri ɗaya.                                                                                                                                  |
| `INPUT_SANITIZER_BLOCK_THRESHOLD` | `high`            | `src/shared/utils/injectionSeverity.ts`  | Mafi ƙarancin matakin tsanani da `MODE=block` zai ƙi: `high` (tsoho), `medium`, ko `low`. Ana sa ido kawai kan tsarukan matsakaicin tsanani sai an rage matakin.                                   |
| `INJECTION_GUARD_BLOCK_THRESHOLD` | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `src/shared/utils/injectionSeverity.ts`  | Tsohon madadin suna na `INPUT_SANITIZER_BLOCK_THRESHOLD` — halayya iri ɗaya.                                                                                                                       |
| `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED`           | `false`           | `src/lib/guardrails/piiMasker.ts`        | Idan `true`, ɓoye PII a cikin buƙatu masu shigowa (ba tare da dogaro da yanayin kutse ba).                                                                                                         |
| `CREDENTIAL_REDACTION_ENABLED`    | `false`           | `src/lib/guardrails/credentialMasker.ts` | Ɓoye sanannun tsarukan API-key / secret-token daga bayanan buƙata/martani. Sai an zaɓa a kunna; yana kwaikwayon `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED`.                                                           |

### Ɓangaren Martani: Mai Tsabtace PII

| Maɓalli                          | Tsoho    | Fayil ɗin Tushe           | Bayani                                                                                 |
| -------------------------------- | -------- | ------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `PII_RESPONSE_SANITIZATION`      | `false`  | `src/lib/piiSanitizer.ts` | Bincika martanin LLM domin gano PII da ya fallasa kafin mayar da shi ga abokin ciniki. |
| `PII_RESPONSE_SANITIZATION_MODE` | `redact` | `src/lib/piiSanitizer.ts` | `redact` = ɓoye PII, `warn` = rubuta log kawai, `block` = watsar da dukkan martanin.   |

### Mai Tsabtace Context na Tokenized-Route na VS Code

| Maɓalli                             | Tsoho | Fayil ɗin Tushe                             | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| ----------------------------------- | ----- | ------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_VSCODE_SANITIZE_CONTEXT` | `1`   | `src/app/api/v1/vscode/contextSanitizer.ts` | Yana cire context na active-editor da aka haɗa kai tsaye (`editorContext`, `activeEditor`, `currentFile`, `selection`, `openTabs`…) daga buƙatun `/v1/vscode/[token]/*` kuma yana ɓoye abubuwan da ke cikin fayiloli masu muhimmanci da aka haɗa a bayyane. Amintacce ta tsoho; saita zuwa `0` domin kashewa. |

### Yanayin Amfani

| Yanayi                    | Saituna                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Bin ƙa'idodin kamfani** | `INPUT_SANITIZER_ENABLED=true`, `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE=block`, `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED=true`, `PII_RESPONSE_SANITIZATION=true` (toshe kutse + ɓoye PII na buƙata/martani; modes ɗin ba su dogara da juna ba) |
| **Sa ido kawai**          | `INPUT_SANITIZER_ENABLED=true`, `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE=warn` — yana rubuta logs amma ba ya taɓa toshewa                                                                                                     |
| **Amfani na kashin kai**  | Bar duk a kashe — babu ƙarin nauyi                                                                                                                                                                         |

---

## 6. Manufofin Kayan Aiki & Routing

| Mai Canji                                                   | Tsoho                               | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                 | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| ----------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `TOOL_POLICY_MODE`                                          | `disabled`                          | `src/lib/toolPolicy.ts`                         | Yana sarrafa damar amfani da kayan aikin LLM/function-calling. `allowlist` = kayan aikin da aka jera kawai, `denylist` = duka sai waɗanda aka jera, `disabled` = babu ƙuntatawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_PAYLOAD_RULES_PATH`                              | `./config/payloadRules.json`        | `open-sse/services/payloadRules.ts`             | Hanyar zuwa fayil ɗin JSON na dokokin sarrafa payload (gyare-gyaren upstream na kowane samfuri/protocol).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_PAYLOAD_RULES_RELOAD_MS`                         | `5000`                              | `open-sse/services/payloadRules.ts`             | Lokacin sake lodi (ms) don sake loda fayil ɗin dokokin payload kai tsaye (hot-reload). Mafi ƙanƙanta `1000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_PREFER_CLAUDE_CODE_FOR_UNPREFIXED_CLAUDE_MODELS` | `false`                             | `open-sse/services/model.ts`                    | Zaɓin shiga na son rai: tura bare `claude-*` model IDs daga abokan cinikin Claude Code ta asusun Claude Code OAuth maimakon buƙatar prefix na mai samarwa. Prefix na mai samarwa da aka bayyana sarai har yanzu suna fifiko. Haka kuma ana iya saita shi ta maɓallin dashboard a shafin mai samar da Claude.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_REDACT_THINKING`                                 | `true`                              | `open-sse/executors/base/redactThinkingBeta.ts` | Saita `false` don hana OmniRoute ƙara Anthropic beta `redact-thinking-2026-02-12` da kanta (blokokin tunani na sa hannu kawai da suke fanko). Buƙatun da ke da `thinking.display: "summarized"` suna ƙetare shi ko ta yaya; beta na redact da abokin ciniki ya yarjejeniya ko CC-compatible `requestDefaults.redactThinking` opt-in suna riƙe shi. Buƙatun Claude OAuth suna riƙe beta (wani ɓangare ne na alamar Claude Code OAuth) sai dai idan an kunna feature flag `CLAUDE_OAUTH_SUMMARIZED_THINKING_UNREDACTED` wanda ake kashewa a tsoho.                                                       |
| `BARE_MODEL_DEFAULT_OVERRIDE_LEGACY`                        | `false`                             | `open-sse/services/model.ts`                    | Halin gargajiya na `resolveBareModelToConnectionDefault` (#4825): maye gurbin kowace sunan samfuri mara prefix da `defaultModel` na haɗin da aka zaɓa. A kashe yake a tsoho, `auto` kawai ko samfuri fanko ake maye gurbinsa; yana rubuta gargaɗi ɗaya ga kowane nau'i biyu idan an kunna shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `COMBO_CONCURRENCY_PER_MODEL`                               | `3`                                 | `open-sse/services/comboConfig.ts`              | Iyakar aiki gungu na kowane samfuri don combos na round-robin (#9100). An ƙuntata semaphore na combo na round-robin a buƙatu 3 masu lokaci guda ga kowane samfuri ba tare da wata hanyar ƙetarewa ba, wanda ya jera zirga-zirgar da ke buƙatar aiki gungu mafi girma a bayan wannan iyaka. An tantance shi zuwa `>= 1`, an ƙuntata shi zuwa `<= 32`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `DISABLE_CONTEXT_WINDOW_CHECKS`                             | `false`                             | `open-sse/handlers/chatCore.ts`                 | Zaɓin shiga mai haɗari wanda ke ƙetare binciken context-window / max-input-token na gida na OmniRoute ga buƙatun samfuri ɗaya kai tsaye. Masu samarwa na upstream har yanzu suna aiwatar da iyakokinsu na ainihi; matsewar prompt da iyakar output-token na samfurin kanta suna ci gaba da aiki. Fifiko na ainihi shine Feature Flags DB override > environment variable > tsoho; ba a buƙatar sake kunna (restart).                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS`                           | _(ba a saita ba)_                   | `open-sse/services/selfHostedEntry.ts`          | Takardar YAML `providers:` a cikin layi (RIC-738, D4). Idan an saita (ko da akwai ɓangaren `strategy:` ko babu), `/v1/chat/completions` yana karkatarwa zuwa shigar da unified OpenAI-compatible ta self-hosted maimakon pipeline na girgije. Ba a saita ba (tsoho): hanyar tana faɗuwa kai tsaye zuwa pipeline na girgije da ake da shi. Duba `docs/routing/SELF_HOSTED_OPENAI_ENTRY.md`.                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS_FILE`                      | _(ba a saita ba)_                   | `open-sse/services/selfHostedEntry.ts`          | Hanyar zuwa fayil ɗin YAML wanda ke riƙe takardar `providers:` iri ɗaya da `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS`, don tursasawa waɗanda suka fi son fayil a kan env var na cikin layi. Ko ɗayansu yana kunna shigar da self-hosted.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_API_KEY`                             | _(ba a saita ba — hanya buɗaɗɗiya)_ | `open-sse/services/selfHostedEntry.ts`          | Maɓallin API na rabawa na zaɓi don shigar da unified self-hosted (D5 scaffold, an kebe shi don tsarin quota na kowane maɓalli). Idan an saita, buƙatu dole ne su haɗa `Authorization: Bearer <key>`. Ba a saita ba: hanyar tana buɗe, daidai da tsarin local-provider na self-hosted da ake da shi (tursasawa na loopback/cibiyar sadarwa da aka amince da ita).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_STRATEGY`                            | _(ba a saita ba)_                   | `open-sse/services/routingStrategies.ts`        | Takaddar YAML `strategy:` na cikin layi don injin tsara hanya na hukunci (M2/RIC-740, D3) — jerin haramtattu/izinattu, mai katse cooldown, fifiko na farashi, mai sanin jinkiri, sarkar madadin. Yana rinjayar toshe `strategy:` na cikin layi da aka saka a cikin `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS`, ga kowane maɓalli. Duba `docs/routing/DETERMINISTIC_ROUTING.md`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
