| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_STRATEGY_FILE`                       | _(ba a saita ba)_                   | `open-sse/services/routingStrategies.ts`        | Hanyar zuwa fayil ɗin YAML wanda ke riƙe da takaddar `strategy:` iri ɗaya kamar `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_STRATEGY`, don aiwatarwa waɗanda suka fi son fayil a kan canjin muhalli na cikin layi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_CONVERSATION_TRACKING`                   | _(ba a saita ba)_                   | `open-sse/services/conversationTracker.ts`      | Saitta `1` don dakatar da tattara tarihin tattaunawa. `resolveConversationId()` yana mayar da sakamako marar bin diddiga kafin ya karanta SQLite ko ya fassara tarihin saƙo, kuma ana rufe ID na zama da abokin ciniki ya bayar. Sarrafa zaman tsara hanya bai canza ba kuma ba a share bayanan da ke akwai ba. Don aiwatarwa waɗanda ba sa amfani da kallon tattaunawa na dashboard kuma suna son teburin juyawa ya daina girma.                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_NADIR_API_KEY`                                   | _(ba a saita ba)_                   | `open-sse/services/autoCombo/nadirStrategy.ts`  | Maɓallin API don dabarar mai tsara hanya ta auto-combo `nadir` da ake shiga da zaɓi, ana amfani da shi lokacin da ba a saita `config.nadir.apiKey` na combo ba. Na zaɓi ne: kira marar maɓalli yana sauka a matakin baƙi na Nadir, wanda aka iyakance yawan kira ga kowane IP kuma saboda haka ba za a iya amfani da shi ba a bayan ƙofa da aka raba. Abu mai sirri; a fi son filin na kowane combo lokacin da combos ke amfani da asusun daban-daban.                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_NADIR_BASE_URL`                                  | `https://api.getnadir.com`          | `open-sse/services/autoCombo/nadirStrategy.ts`  | Asalin tushe na API na yanke shawara na Nadir, ana amfani da shi lokacin da ba a saita `config.nadir.baseUrl` na combo ba. Ana buƙatarsa ne kawai don Nadir da ake karɓar bakuncinsa da kai, wanda ke riƙe kanun tambayoyi a cikin cibiyar sadarwarku. Ana jure `/v1` na ƙarshe kuma ana cire shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_WEEKLY_WINDOW`                           | `provider`                          | `src/lib/usage/apiKeyUsageLimits.ts`            | Tagar mako-mako don iyakokin amfani da USD na kowane maɓallin API (tilasta buƙatu, `GET /api/keys/{id}/usage-limits`, `@@om-usage`). `provider`: mako yana ƙarewa a farkon sake saitin mako-mako daga cikin haɗin gwiwar sama da maɓallin zai iya kaiwa, ko kuma kwanaki 7 masu juyawa ne lokacin da babu wanda aka sani. `calendar`: mako na gida daga Litinin 00:00 zuwa Litinin na gaba 00:00, iri ɗaya ga kowane maɓalli. Ana ba da shawarar `calendar` lokacin da maɓallai da yawa masu kasafin kuɗi na daidaikun suke raba rukuni ɗaya na asusun sama. Ƙimar marar inganci tana koma `provider`. |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_WEEKLY_WINDOW_TIMEZONE`                  | _(yankin lokacin tsari)_            | `src/lib/usage/apiKeyUsageLimits.ts`            | Yankin lokaci na IANA (misali `Asia/Shanghai`) wanda ke ayyana Litinin 00:00 don tagar mako-mako ta `calendar`. Ba a saita ba ko marar inganci: yankin lokaci na tsarin OmniRoute (`TZ` na mai karɓar bakunci ko kwandon, wanda sau da yawa ba a saita ba, watau Lokacin Duniya na Haɗaka, a cikin hotunan kwandon). Ana watsi da shi a yanayin `provider`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_MAX_TOOLS_LIMIT`                                 | `128`                               | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                  | Mafi yawan kayan aikin da aka yarda a cikin buƙata ta tsoho (tsoho na OpenAI). Lambobi cikakku masu kyau kawai; ba a saita ba ko ƙimar marar inganci tana riƙe `128`. Mai samarwa da iyaka da aka sani ko aka gabatar (`grok-cli` 200, `nvidia` 1536) har yanzu yana aiwatar da iyakarsa — wannan yana matsar da mafi yawan tsoho kawai.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROMPT_EMULATE_TOOLS`                            | _(kunna)_                           | `open-sse/executors/syntxChat.ts`               | Kiran kayan aiki da aka kwaikwaya ta tambaya na SYNTX.ai: ba a saita ba ko `1`/`true`/`on`/`yes` yana shigar da katalogin kayan aiki na gida a cikin tambaya; kowace ƙima dabam (misali `0`) tana tsallake wannan shigarwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

Idan ba a saita ba, ana amfani da tsohuwar ƙimar matakin kyauta `opencode,opencode-go,opencode-cli`; `none`/`off`/`false`/`0` yana kashe wannan cascade gaba ɗaya; jerin da aka raba da waƙafi yana maye gurbin saitin. Shirye-shiryen biya ana haɗa su bisa asusu a upstream kuma za su iya kashe shi cikin aminci (#9611).                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |

Idan ba a saita ba, ana ware memba na pool bayan 429 ne kawai ga dangin da aka haɗa bisa IP da ke sama; `all` yana faɗaɗa wannan rubutawa zuwa kowane mai bayarwa, ta yadda rate limit da aka samu ta wani memba zai sa zaɓi na gaba ya yi egress ta wani wuri. Ana yin watsi da kowace ƙima da ba `all` ba. Yana buƙatar `PROXY_SKIP_RECENTLY_FAILED` (yana kunne ta tsohuwa). An tsara store ɗin bisa adireshin egress, ba bisa (mai bayarwa, adireshi) ba, don haka faɗaɗa 429 yana dakatar da wannan memba ga kowane mai bayarwa har sai lokacin ya wuce (minti 5, yana ninkawa zuwa 15). Ana karanta shi ga kowane sakamakon amsa, don haka sauyi yana aiki ba tare da sake kunnawa ba. |

Idan babu komai, ana komawa ga placeholder guda ɗaya da ake gaya wa model kada ya kira. Aƙalla shigarwa 32, `[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_-]{0,63}`; ana yin watsi da shigarwar da ba su inganta ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |

Mai muhimmanci; fi son filin Dashboard na kowane haɗi idan an saita asusu da yawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OLLAMA_CLOUD_USAGE_COOKIE`                 | _(ba a saita ba)_                                         | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | Madadin canjin muhalli na cookie na Ollama Cloud `__Secure-session`. Mai muhimmanci; fi son filin Dashboard na kowane haɗi idan an saita asusu da yawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_OLLAMA_USAGE_COOKIE`             | _(ba a saita ba)_                                         | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | Madadin canjin muhalli na kuki `__Secure-session` na Ollama Cloud wanda ake amfani da shi kafin gajerun laƙabban. Yana da muhimmancin tsaro; idan an saita asusu da yawa, fi son filin Dashboard na kowace haɗi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEWHISPERER_BASE_URL`          | `https://codewhisperer.us-east-1.amazonaws.com`           | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | Maƙurar iyakokin amfani ta CodeWhisperer (AWS Kiro). Sauya ta don relay / kayan gwaji.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |

> [!IMPORTANT]
> Lokacin turawa a bayan reverse proxy (nginx, Caddy), saita `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL` zuwa tsayayyen URL ɗinka na jama'a (misali, `https://omniroute.example.com`) idan kiraye-kirayen baya na OAuth ko hanyoyin jama'a da aka samar dole ne su yi amfani da wannan sunan masaukin. Idan babu wannan, kiraye-kirayen baya na OAuth na iya gaza saboda redirect_uri ba zai dace ba, kuma hanyoyin jama'a da aka samar za su iya nuna asalin container na ciki.
>
> Bar `BASE_URL` a matsayin URL na loopback/container na ciki don ayyukan uwar-garke-zuwa-uwar-garke. Kada a yi amfani da `Origin` na burauza ko sunan masaukin jama'a don buƙatun ciki da tsarin ke aika wa kansa waɗanda ke ɗauke da bayanan shaidar shiga.
>
> Ayyukan rubutu na dashboard da aka tantance ba sa buƙatar tsayayyen URL na jama'a: dashboard ɗin yana aika buƙatun da ba su da aminci na asali ɗaya tare da token na CSRF da ke daure da zaman. Har yanzu OmniRoute yana tattara tabbatar da asalin jama'a wuri guda don haɗa-hadar burauza waɗanda ba na dashboard ba: da farko ana amincewa da bayyanannun canje-canjen muhalli na URL na jama'a; ana yin watsi da ɗanyen headers na `Forwarded` / `X-Forwarded-*` sai idan an kunna `OMNIROUTE_TRUST_PROXY` kuma an yi wa takwaran proxy na kai tsaye alama da token a matsayin abin dogaro. Kada a yi amfani da saitunan CORS don gyara buƙatun dashboard na asali ɗaya; CORS na abokan hulɗar burauza na ƙetare-asali ne kawai.

---

## 8. Proxy na Fita

Yi wa kiran masu samar da LLM na sama (upstream) hanya ta wata HTTP ko SOCKS5 proxy domin sarrafa fita zuwa waje, tsara hanyoyin yanki, ko ɓoye adreshin IP.

`OMNIROUTE_UPSTREAM_HTTP2_ENABLED` tana da tsoffin ƙima `true`. Saita ita zuwa `false`, `0`, `no`, ko
`off` domin tilasta HTTP/1.1 a kan dispatchers na Undici a cikin `open-sse/utils/proxyDispatcher.ts`
da `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`: fita kai tsaye, proxies na HTTP(S)/SOCKS, relays, da
sake gwadawarsu ta sababbin sockets. Wannan yana kiyaye tabbatar da takardar shaidar TLS, tantancewar
proxy, iyakokin haɗi, da saitunan keep-alive. Sake ƙaddamar da OmniRoute bayan
canza ta saboda ana adana dispatchers a cache kuma ana ƙirƙirar relay agents a lokacin ƙaddamarwa.
Wannan maɓallin ba ya sarrafa wreq TLS fingerprinting ko transports na musamman na kowane mai samarwa.

| Mai canji                                | Asali             | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| ---------------------------------------- | ----------------- | ---------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY`                    | `true`            | `open-sse/executors`                           | Kunna wakilin SOCKS5 proxy don kiran upstream. A fice da shi da `false`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY`        | `true`            | Bangaren abokin ciniki                         | Sanin bangaren abokin ciniki game da samuwar SOCKS5.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `PROXY_SKIP_RECENTLY_FAILED`             | `true`            | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`             | Tutar fasali da ke kunne ta asali (dubi [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md); wani override na dashboard DB yana cin nasara). Proxy pools da juyawar kowane asusu suna daina sake amfani da memba wanda ya yi gazawa kwanan nan (wanda ya ƙi amsa binciken TCP, ko 429 da ta shiga ta hannunsa) na wani lokaci wanda ke ninkin biyu a kowane maimaitawa, har zuwa iyaka. `false` yana maida zaɓin yau da kullum.                                                                |
| `SELECTOR_CONTROL_ALLOWLIST`             | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `src/lib/proxySubscription/selectorGuard.ts`   | Jerin izini don manufofin sarrafa-selector na local-core bayan loopback (masauki ko adiresoshin IP da aka raba da waƙafi ko sarari, `*` yana buɗe LAN/private amma ba a taɓa buɗe metadata/link-local). Ana izina loopback a kan http/https ta asali. Idan an saita daraja a dashboard DB kuma ba fanko ba, tana cin nasara a kan wannan variable.                                                                                                                                |
| `PROXY_QUOTA_429_BASE_MS`                | `300000`          | `open-sse/utils/proxyRefusalMemory.ts`         | Lokacin asali (ms) na keɓewar 429, wanda ke ninkin biyu a kowane maimaitawa. Ana karanta shi sau ɗaya lokacin ƙaddamarwa. An takaita shi zuwa `1000..3600000`; idan ya wuce iyaka ko ba a iya karanta shi ba, yana komawa na asali tare da gargaɗi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `PROXY_QUOTA_429_MAX_MS`                 | `900000`          | `open-sse/utils/proxyRefusalMemory.ts`         | Iyaka (ms) ta keɓewar 429. Ana karanta shi sau ɗaya lokacin ƙaddamarwa. An takaita shi zuwa `1000..3600000`; idan ya wuce iyaka ko ba a iya karanta shi ba, yana komawa na asali tare da gargaɗi. Idan iyakar ta kasance ƙarƙashin asalin, tana komawa na asali. Rage iyakar yana gajarta manta jerin gazawa: ana share shigarwar `2 x max` bayan ƙarewa.                                                                                                                         |
| `PROXY_POOL_SHARED_EGRESS_ORDER`         | `false`           | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`             | Tutar fasali ta zaɓin-kunna (dubi [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md); wani override na dashboard DB yana cin nasara). Ga masu samarwa waɗanda aka kwatance kuotar su bisa adireshin fita (egress), sanya memba na pool wanda ke raba adireshin fita da aka lura da shi na memba da aka ƙi kwanan nan a matsayi kaɗan ƙasa da mambobi masu lafiya. Tsari kawai, ba a taɓa keɓe su. Yana buƙatar `PROXY_SKIP_RECENTLY_FAILED`, wanda ke samar da siginar ƙin da yake karantawa. |
| `PROXY_WEBHOOK_REBOUND_MS`               | `300000`          | `src/lib/proxyEvents/proxyTransitionBridge.ts` | Tagar dawowa (ms) wadda ke takaita maimaita sanarwar webhook na `proxy.set_aside` ga kowane memba na pool, domin tarƙon ƙin yarda ya aika lamari ɗaya kawai. Ana karanta shi a kowane fitarwa. An takaita shi zuwa `1000..3600000`; idan ya wuce iyaka ko ba a iya karanta shi ba, yana komawa na asali a shiru.                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `HTTP_PROXY`                             | _(ba a saita ba)_ | Daidaitaccen Node.js                           | HTTP proxy don kiran upstream.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `HTTPS_PROXY`                            | _(ba a saita ba)_ | Daidaitaccen Node.js                           | HTTPS proxy don kiran upstream.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `ALL_PROXY`                              | _(ba a saita ba)_ | Daidaitaccen Node.js                           | Proxy gama-gari (yana goyon bayan `socks5://`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_ECHO_URL`               | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `src/lib/proxyEchoTarget.ts`                   | Yana kafa manufar echo-IP da binciken fitowar proxy (egress) yake amfani da ita zuwa URL guda ɗaya. Idan ba a saita ba, binciken yana gwada `api64.ipify.org` sannan `api4.ipify.org` domin kada a yi rahoton cewa layukan IPv4-kawai sun mutu (#9694).                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `NO_PROXY`                               | _(ba a saita ba)_ | Daidaitaccen Node.js                           | Sunayen masaukin/IP da aka raba da waƙafi don wuce proxy.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_DISPATCHER_CONNECTIONS` | `32`              | `open-sse/utils/proxyDispatcher.ts`            | Mafi yawan sockets masu aiki a lokaci ɗaya ga kowanne dispatcher na proxy na HTTP/SOCKS da aka ajiye a cache. Dogayen rafukan SSE kamar Codex `/v1/responses` suna buƙatar fiye da haɗi ɗaya idan buƙatu da yawa suna amfani da proxy ɗaya na matakin asusu. Ana ƙuntata ƙimar da ta haura `256`.                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `SOCKS_HANDSHAKE_TIMEOUT_MS`             | `10000`           | `open-sse/utils/socksConnectorWithFamily.ts`   | Lokacin ƙarewa (timeout) na handshake (haɗi) na SOCKS5 a cikin ms. Ku ɗaga shi idan masauki ɗaya na ƙofar zama ya fuskanci matsananciyar yawan buƙatu a lokaci ɗaya (misali 100 buƙatu a lokaci ɗaya) — ainihin handshake na iya wuce 10s a ƙarƙashin cika-cikin pool ko da yake ana iya isa proxy, wanda in ba haka ba zai bayyana a matsayin ƙarya `[Proxy Fast-Fail] Proxy unreachable`. An ƙuntata shi a `120000`.                                                            |
| `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN`                        | `false`           | `src/sse/handlers/chatHelpers.ts`              | Idan `false` (na asali), ana **ƙin (fail-closed)** buƙata wadda proxy da aka sanya mata ta kasa warwarewa maimakon faɗowa zuwa haɗin kai tsaye — hakan yana hana zubowar ainihin IP. Saita `true` don maido da tsohon DIRECT fallback.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `ENABLE_TLS_FINGERPRINT`                 | `false`           | `open-sse/executors`                           | Gurbata TLS fingerprint ta amfani da wreq-js (yana kwaikwayon Chrome 124). Yana mayar da martani ga toshewar JA3/JA4.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `TLS_FINGERPRINT_PROVIDERS`              | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`                 | Jerin izinin masu samarwa da aka raba da waƙafi don sabon jagorar TLS ta proxy (`open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`). TLS na kai tsaye yana riƙe tsohon halinsa idan ba a saita ba; kawai waɗannan masu samarwa ne suke wucewa ta gadar fingerprint ta Chrome-124. An fitar da Groq (`api.groq.com`) ko da lokacin da ba a saita jerin izinin ba, saboda Chrome JA3 yana haifar da Cloudflare 1010 yayin da undici na asali yana isa API.                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_TURNSTILE_IGNORE_TLS_ERRORS`  | `false`           | `open-sse/services/claudeTurnstileSolver.ts`   | Bari mahallin burauzar Playwright na Claude Turnstile ya yi watsi da kurakuran takaddun shaida na HTTPS.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

### Yanayi

| Yanayi                                                   | Tsari                                                                                                                     |
| -------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **SOCKS5 ta hanyar layin SSH**                           | `ALL_PROXY=socks5://127.0.0.1:7890`, `ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY=true`                                                           |
| **Proxy na HTTP na kamfani**                             | `HTTP_PROXY=http://proxy.corp.com:3128`, `HTTPS_PROXY=http://proxy.corp.com:3128`, `NO_PROXY=localhost,internal.corp.com` |
| **Yaƙi da fingerprint**                                  | `ENABLE_TLS_FINGERPRINT=true` — yana buƙatar `wreq-js` (an haɗa shi)                                                      |
| **Egress da ake sarrafawa / babu damar kai tsaye**       | Bar `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN=false` (na asali). Buƙatu suna gaza ƙwarai idan proxy ba ya samuwa maimakon zubo ta haɗin kai tsaye. |
| **Tsohon tsari / ci gaba — barin fallback na kai tsaye** | `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN=true`. Yana maido da halin kafin ƙarfafawa: ana amfani da haɗin kai tsaye idan warware proxy ya gaza.    |

> **Lura (wucewar ingantaccen NVIDIA — #3226):** Ƙarshen inganta maɓallin API na NVIDIA
> yana tsayawa idan aka jagoranta shi ta hanyar fetch na duniya wanda aka dafa shi da proxy/TLS (undici dispatcher → 504).
> `src/lib/providers/validation.ts::directHttpsRequest()` yana da nufin wuce patch na proxy ga kiran ingantaccen ɗaya nan ta amfani da `safeOutboundFetch({ bypassProxyPatch: true })`.
> Wannan keɓancewa ne da aka rubuta kuma aka iyakance — **ba** ya shafar fitowar chat/amfani.
> Ana ɗaure ikon wucewa ta `tests/unit/proxy-bypass-scope-guard-3226.test.ts`.

---

## 9. Haɗin Kayayyakin CLI

Yana sarrafa yadda OmniRoute ke gano da ƙaddamar da CLI sidecars (Claude Code, Codex, da sauransu).

| Mabambanta                        | Asali                            | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                     | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| --------------------------------- | -------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CLI_MODE`                        | `auto`                           | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | `auto` = nemo a cikin PATH na tsarin; `manual` = yi amfani da hanyoyin da aka bayyana kawai.                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `CLI_EXTRA_PATHS`                 | _(ba a saita ba)_                | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ƙarin shigarwar PATH don gano binary na CLI (an raba su da colon).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `CLI_CONFIG_HOME`                 | _(ba a saita ba)_                | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Maye gurbin kundin gida don karanta configs na CLI (`~/.claude`, `~/.codex`). Dole ne ya kasance cikakkiyar hanya (absolute) kuma a cikin gidan process — **ko**, a cikin container, hanya da aka yi bind-mount (wannan shine yadda `/host-home` ke aiki). Duk wani abu dabam yana komawa zuwa kundin gida. |
| `CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES`         | `true`                           | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Bari OmniRoute ya rubuta fayilolin config na CLI (sabuntawar token, bayanan session). Yana daidaita da canjin muhalli da mai shigar da Hermes PowerShell ke saita a Windows (`%LOCALAPPDATA%\hermes`).                                                                                                           |

### Daidaitawar Profile na CLI ta Atomatik

Waɗannan tutocin fasali na zaɓi ne kuma tsoffin saitinsu a kashe suke. Haka kuma ana iya
canza su daga allon sarrafa CLI Code.

| Canji                                 | Tsoho   | Fayil na Tushe                                   | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| ------------------------------------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OMNIROUTE_AUTO_SYNC_CODEX_PROFILES`  | `false` | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts` | Bayan daidaita samfurin mai samarwa, yana sake rubuta fayilolin profile `~/.codex/*.config.toml` daga ƙasaɗa (catalog) mai rai a kai tsaye. Yana buƙatar `CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES`; ba ya taɓa canza saitin Codex mai aiki/tsoho, tantancewa, saitunan Codex-lb, ko zaɓin mai samarwa.     |
| `OMNIROUTE_AUTO_SYNC_CLAUDE_PROFILES` | `false` | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts` | Bayan daidaita samfurin mai samarwa, yana sake rubuta fayilolin profile na Claude Code `~/.claude/profiles/<name>/settings.json` daga ƙasaɗa mai rai a kai tsaye. Yana buƙatar `CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES`; ba ya taɓa canza saitin Claude mai aiki/tsoho, tantancewa, ko zaɓin mai samarwa. |

### Misalin Docker

```bash
# Shigar da binary na mai masauki a cikin container kuma gaya wa OmniRoute inda suke:
CLI_EXTRA_PATHS=/host-cli/bin
CLI_CONFIG_HOME=/host-home
CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES=true
CLI_CLAUDE_BIN=/host-cli/bin/claude
```

`CLI_CONFIG_HOME` yana aiki ne kawai idan an yi bind-mount na hanyar daga mai masauki (host) — haɗa shi da mounts kamar `~/.codex:/host-home/.codex:rw` (duba `host` profile a cikin `docker-compose.yml`). Ana watsi da hanya da ba ta cikin gidan mai amfani na container ba kuma ba ta bind mount ba, domin rubutawa a can za a jefar da ita sa'ad da aka sake ƙirƙirar container.

Hoton yana gudana a matsayin `USER node`, don haka `/root` da ba a mount ba **ba** ingantaccen override ba ne.

| Mai Canji                                | Tsoho         | Fayil ɗin Tushe                     | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| ---------------------------------------- | ------------- | ----------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTAINER`                    | _(kai-tsaye)_ | `src/shared/utils/containerEnv.ts`  | Ƙarfafa gano container a kunna (`1`/`true`) ko a kashe (`0`/`false`). Ana buƙatarsa ne kawai a runtimes da gano kai-tsaye bai isa ba.                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_CONTAINER_CONFIG_WRITE` | `false`       | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts` | Ƙyale rubutun config na kayan aikin CLI zuwa hanyar container da ba a mount ba duk da haka. Daidaitaccen abu a CLI shi ne `--allow-container-write`.                                                                                                                                   |
| `DOCKER_UPDATE_TRIGGER_FILE`             | ba a saita ba | `src/lib/system/autoUpdate.ts`      | Hoton Docker kawai: dashboard **Sabunta Yanzu** yana rubuta buƙatar JSON zuwa wannan fayil (dole ya kasance cikin `DATA_DIR`) domin mai sa ido na gefen host ya yi pull kuma ya sake ƙirƙirar container. Duba [Jagorar Docker](../guides/DOCKER_GUIDE.md#dashboard-updates-in-docker). |

### Masu taimako na Binary na CLI (`omniroute`)

Waɗannan masu canji suna daidaita halayen binary na CLI na `omniroute` da kansa (ba gano sidecar da ke sama ba).

| Mai Canji                             | Tsoho                             | Fayil ɗin Tushe                         | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ------------------------------------- | --------------------------------- | --------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OMNIROUTE_LANG`                      | _(tsarin)_                        | `bin/cli/i18n.mjs`                      | Ƙarfafa harshen fitarwa na CLI. BCP-47 locale (misali `en`, `pt-BR`). Yana rinjayar masu canjin locale na tsarin (LC_ALL, LC_MESSAGES).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_SHOW_LOG`                  | _(ba a saita ba)_                 | `bin/cli/runtime/processSupervisor.mjs` | Saita zuwa `1` domin aika stdout/stderr na uwar garken zuwa terminal a yanayin kula (supervised mode). Daidai da tutar `--log` a `omniroute serve`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLI_TOKEN`                 | _(ba a saita ba)_                 | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | Token na tantance na'ura (machine-auth) da ake saka a matsayin header `x-omniroute-cli-token`. Ana ƙirƙirar shi kai-tsaye a aikin 8.12.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `GEMINI_CLI_HOME`                     | _(ba a saita ba)_                 | `bin/cli/commands/setup-gemini.mjs`     | Tushen gida na Gemini na dindindin; gudanarwa ta sau ɗaya (one-shot) tana amfani da gida na wucin gadi da aka ware.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHILD_SIGNAL_TIMEOUT_MS`   | `5000`                            | `bin/cli/commands/run.mjs`              | Lokacin jinkiri (grace period) a millisecond kafin a tilasta kashe child process na CLI da aka aika sigina.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLI_SKIP_DEFAULT_DATA_ENV` | _(ba a saita ba)_                 | `bin/omniroute.mjs`                     | Saita zuwa 1 tare da DATA_DIR da aka fayyace domin tsallake fayil ɗin muhalli na kundin bayanan tsohon mai amfani.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_SYNC_TOKEN`                | _(ba a saita ba)_                 | `bin/cli/commands/sync.mjs`             | Token na daidaitawa (sync) don `omniroute sync bundle` (`GET /api/sync/bundle` yana karɓar token na daidaitawa kawai, ba maɓallin API ba). Daidai da `--token`.                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_HTTP_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `30000`                           | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | Ƙarewar lokacin HTTP (timeout) ga kowane yunƙuri (ms) don buƙatun CLI → uwar garken.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_READY_TIMEOUT_MS`          | `60000`                           | `bin/cli/utils/pid.mjs`                 | Mafi girman lokaci (ms) da CLI yake jira don health endpoint na uwar garken kafin ya buga gargaɗin ƙarewar lokaci. Yana da amfani ga cold starts masu jinkiri (misali Windows). Hakanan ana iya saita shi ta `--ready-timeout`.                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_VERBOSE`                   | `0`                               | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | Saita zuwa `1` domin buga binciken retry/backoff zuwa stderr yayin umarnin CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_PLUGIN_PATH`               | _(ba a saita ba)_                 | `bin/cli/plugins.mjs`                   | Kundin da aka keɓance don gano plugins na CLI (paketeji `omniroute-cmd-*`). Tsoho shi ne `~/.omniroute/plugins/` idan ba a saita ba. CLI kawai — bai taba kai wa mai duba plugins na gefen uwar garken ba, wanda `OMNIROUTE_PLUGINS_DIR` yake nuna (sashe na 2).                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_NO_UPDATE_NOTIFIER`        | _(ba a saita ba)_                 | `bin/omniroute.mjs`                     | Hana sanarwar sabuntawa na CLI lokacin da tsarin yake fita idan an saita zuwa kowace ƙima da ba fanko ba. Sanarwar tana ci gaba da kasancewa a kashe daban a CI, yanayin shiru, da yanayin fitarwa da ba rubutu ba.                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_DOCTOR_LIVENESS_URL`       | _(an samo)_                       | `bin/cli/commands/doctor.mjs`           | URL na liveness da `omniroute doctor` yake gwada. Zaɓin `livenessUrl` na shiri (programmatic) yana da fifiko; in ba haka ba ana amfani da wannan ƙimar kamar yadda yake. Idan ba a saita ba, CLI yana samun endpoint `/api/health/degradation` na dashboard na gida.                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_CATALOG_PATH`     | `src/shared/constants/providers/` | `bin/cli/provider-catalog.mjs`          | Hanyar katalog na masu samarwa da umarnin CLI na masu samarwa suke amfani da ita. Zaɓin `catalogPath` na shiri yana da fifiko. Ana amfani da absolute paths kamar yadda suke; relative paths suna warwarewa daga tushen aikin. Idan ba a saita ba, CLI yana amfani da kundin tsayayyun ƙimomi na masu samarwa, tare da fayil ɗin gado `providers.ts` a matsayin madadin. |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_TEST_MODEL`       | _(tsohon mai samarwa)_            | `bin/cli/provider-test.mjs`             | Ƙirar madadin duniya don gwaje-gwajen haɗi na masu samarwa na CLI. Fifiko shi ne ƙirar haɗi da aka saita, override na ƙirar musamman mai samarwa, wannan ƙima, sannan ƙirar gwaji da aka gina cikin mai samarwa.                                                                                                                                                         |
