## 10. Haɗin Agent na Ciki & MCP

| Mai Canji                                       | Tsoho                                               | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                             | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| ----------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_BASE_URL`                            | ganowa ta atomatik                                  | `open-sse/mcp-server/server.ts`                             | URL na musamman don kayan aikin MCP/A2A su isa OmniRoute. Yana ƙetare ganowa ta localhost ta atomatik.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY`                             | _(ba a saita ba)_                                   | Modulolin MCP/A2A                                           | Mabuɗin API don kiran kayan aikin MCP na ciki da ƙwarewar A2A.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_ID`                          | _(ba a saita ba)_                                   | `open-sse/mcp-server/audit.ts`                              | ID na mabuɗi don danganta log na binciken MCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `ROUTER_API_KEY`                                | _(ba a saita ba)_                                   | Tsoho                                                       | Lakabin tsoho don `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_A2A_HISTORY_RETENTION_DAYS`          | `30`                                                | `src/lib/a2a/taskManager.ts`                                | Kwanakin tarihin aikin A2A da ake ajiyewa a cikin database na gida kafin sharewar kowace rana ta share jere. Mara saiti, mara lamba, ko `<= 0` yana komawa `30`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_A2A_MEMORY_HITS`                     | `1`                                                 | `src/lib/a2a/taskExecution.ts`                              | Kill-switch don fasalin lura da memory-hits na A2A. Saita zuwa `0` don tsallake neman tunawa da memory gaba ɗaya don wani aiki; kowace wata ƙima (ciki har da ba a saita ba) tana barin shi a kunne.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_ISSUE_AGENT_ENABLED`                 | `false`                                             | `src/app/api/issue-agent/runs/route.ts`                     | Yana kunna endpoint na recorded-triage na Issue Agent na offline/gida. Bar shi a kashe sai dai idan kana gudanar da ayyukan recorded-triage na gida a fili.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_ISSUE_AGENT_TIMEOUT_MS`              | _(ba a saita ba)_                                   | `src/lib/issueAgent/execution.ts`                           | Lokacin ƙarewa (ms) don gudanar da recorded-triage ɗaya na Issue Agent. An takaita shi zuwa matsakaicin ciki; yana komawa tsohon da aka gina shi idan ba a saita ba ko ba daidai ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTEXT`                             | _(mahallin da yake aiki)_                           | `bin/cli/program.mjs`, `bin/cli/api.mjs`                    | Mahallin/profile na yanayin nesa na CLI don umarnin `omniroute`; yana ƙetare mahallin da yake aiki a cikin ajiyar mahallin gida. Daidai da `--context <name>`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTEXT_KEYCHAIN_DISABLED`           | `0`                                                 | `bin/cli/contexts.mjs`                                      | Kashe backend na keychain na OS na `keytar` na zaɓi don takardun shaidar mahallin CLI. Lokacin da aka kunna, takardun shaida suna ci gaba da kasancewa a cikin `config.json` mode `0600` kuma CLI yana fitar da gargaɗin fallback na sau ɗaya; an yi niyyar shi don gudanar da headless/container da gangan.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_ENFORCE_SCOPES`                  | `false`                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/server.ts`                             | Aiwatar da sarrafa dama bisa scope akan kiran kayan aikin MCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_SCOPES`                          | _(duka)_                                            | `open-sse/mcp-server/server.ts`                             | Scopes da aka raba da waƙafi: `admin`, `combos`, `health`, `models`, `routing`, `budget`, `metrics`, `pricing`, `memory`, `skills`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_COMPRESS_DESCRIPTIONS`           | `false`                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/descriptionCompressor.ts`              | Matsa bayanan kayan aikin MCP kafin jera manifest. Ƙimar kunna: `1`, `true`, `on`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_DESCRIPTION_COMPRESSION`         | `rtk`                                               | `open-sse/mcp-server/descriptionCompressor.ts`              | Algorithm/profile na matsawa. Ƙimar kashewa: `0`, `false`, `off`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                | `10000`                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/fetchTimeout.ts`                       | Ƙasafin lokacin fashewa (ms) don karatun gudanarwa na ciki na MCP-server (lafiya, juriya, combos, quota, amfani).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_UPSTREAM_TIMEOUT_MS`             | `60000`                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/fetchTimeout.ts`                       | Kasafin lokacin yanke aiki (ms) na matakan MCP da suke jiran mai bada sabis (`route_request`, `web_search`, `web_fetch`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_CORPUS_CACHE_SIZE`                   | `5`                                                 | `src/lib/localCorpus/configured.ts`                         | Mafi girman adadin misalan index na local-corpus da ake adanawa a ƙwaƙwalwa (LRU, ɗaya ga kowane babban directory da aka yi index). An takaita zuwa mafi ƙarancin `1`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `MODEL_SYNC_INTERVAL_HOURS`                     | `6`                                                 | `src/shared/services/modelSyncScheduler.ts`                 | Tsaikon daidaita jerin samfura (model catalog) a awanni.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `PROVIDER_LIMITS_SYNC_INTERVAL_MINUTES`         | `70`                                                | `src/lib/usage/providerLimits.ts`                           | Tsaikon binciken iyakokin yawan buƙatu da quota na mai bada sabis.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `PROVIDER_LIMITS_SYNC_SPACING_MS`               | `1500`                                              | `src/lib/usage/providerLimits.ts`                           | Tsangwama (ms) tsakanin jawo quota na OAuth da ke jere a cikin daidaitawa ta taro; ana jawo haɗin OAuth ɗaya bayan ɗaya don guje wa motsa ikkisarwa ga upstream. `0` yana ficewa daga wannan (aiki tare).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_QUOTA_FETCH_MIN_INTERVAL_MS`         | `250`                                               | `open-sse/services/quotaFetchThrottle.ts`                   | Mafi ƙarancin tsaiko (ms) tsakanin jawo quota na upstream da ke jere a kan hanyar preflight/monitor ta kowace buƙata; yana raba kiran cibiyar sadarwa da suke gudana tare domin asusun da yawa a kan IP ɗaya kada su motsa upstream ta ikkisarwa. An haɗa shi cikin masu jawo quota na Codex (`/wham/usage`), DeepSeek, Bailian (dukan wuraren jawo biyu), OpenCode, da Crof (#6009, #6911). Hanyar dispatch ta gama-gari ta `usage.ts::getUsageForProvider` (github/glm/minimax/nanogpt/xai/etc.) ba a rufe ta ba tukuna — ana bin ta a daban. Bugun cache ba shi da tasiri. `0` yana kashewa; an takaita `0..60000`. |
| `OMNIROUTE_QUOTA_FETCH_JITTER_MS`               | `120`                                               | `open-sse/services/quotaFetchThrottle.ts`                   | Ƙarin tsangwama bazuwar (ms) ta kowace buƙata, wadda ake zaɓta a kowane jawo quota na upstream da aka kiyaye. Tana 120 tuni; an takaita `0..60000`; `0` yana cire jitter kawai. Saita `OMNIROUTE_QUOTA_FETCH_MIN_INTERVAL_MS=0` don kashe dukan throttlen. Jitter ba ta tabbatar da hana haramtawa daga upstream ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `PROVIDER_LIMITS_POST_USAGE_REFRESH_DELAY_MS`   | `5000`                                              | `src/lib/usage/providerLimits.ts`                           | Jinkiri (ms) kafin sabunta iyakokin mai bada sabis bayan ainihin lamarin amfani, yana bawa quota API na upstream lokacin yin rajistar cinye.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOGIN_BROWSER_PATH`                  | ganowa ta atomatik                                  | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts`             | Cikakkiyar hanya zuwa executable na Chrome ko Edge na tsari wanda ake amfani da shi don shiga Adobe Firefly ta hanyar hulɗa da sabuntawa ba a kan allo ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BROWSER_REFRESH`                 | an kunna                                            | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                  | Yana kiyaye yanayin IMS da na haɗarin browser sabo tare da zama na Chrome CDP da aka keɓe ga asusu. Saita zuwa `0` don kashe sabuntawar browser.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_SESSION_DISK`                    | an kunna                                            | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                  | Yana adana zama na Adobe da aka gyara a ƙarƙashin `DATA_DIR` a duk sake kunna tsari. Saita zuwa `0` don kiyaye zama a ƙwaƙwalwa kawai.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_MIN_SUBMIT_GAP_MS`               | `12000`                                             | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                  | Mafi ƙarancin tsangwama a milliseconds tsakanin ƙaddamar da ayyukan ƙirƙira na Adobe Firefly; `0` yana kashe tsangwama.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BATCH_EXTRA_GAP_MS`              | `15000`                                             | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                  | Ƙarin lokacin hutu a milliseconds bayan kowace ƙaddamarwa ta Adobe ta uku da ta yi nasara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_SUBMIT_BASE_DELAY_MS`            | `8000`                                              | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyClient.ts`                   | Tushen jinkirin sake gwadawa a milliseconds bayan amsoshin Adobe 408 na ɗan lokaci; an haɗa shi da tsangwamar ƙaddamarwa a cikin fannoni biyar mafi yawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_BACKGROUND_SERVICES`         | `false`                                             | `src/instrumentation-node.ts`                               | Kashe duk ayyukan bango (daidaitawa, farashi, sabunta samfura). Yana da amfani ga CI/test.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_HEADLESS`                            | _(ba a saita ba)_                                   | `src/lib/system/headless.ts`                                | Farawa da injin proxy kawai: yana tsallake ƙananan tsarin da za a iya barinsu, `/dashboard` → 404. Daidai da `omniroute serve --headless`. Duba [HEADLESS.md](../guides/HEADLESS.md).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_ENABLE_RUNTIME_BACKGROUND_TASKS`     | _(ba a saita ba)_                                   | `src/lib/config/runtimeSettings.ts`                         | Tilasta kunna ayyukan bango lokacin gano gwaji na atomatik. Saita `1` don ƙetare hanyar kimantawa ta gwaji.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_BUDGET_RESET_JOB_INTERVAL_MS`        | `600000`                                            | `src/lib/jobs/budgetResetJob.ts`                            | Matsayin lokacin duba sake saita kasafin kuɗi (ms). Mafi ƙanƙanta `10000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONNECTION_RECOVERY_INTERVAL_MS`     | `60000`                                             | `src/lib/quota/connectionRecovery.ts`                       | Matsayin lokacin murmurewa na sanyaya-haɗi (ms): yana sake tantance haɗin da lokutan `rate_limited_until` na wucin gadi suka shuɗe, a wajen hanyar buƙata mai zafi. Mafi ƙanƙanta `5000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_CONNECTION_RECOVERY`         | `false`                                             | `src/lib/quota/connectionRecovery.ts`                       | Kashe tsarin tsarawa na murmurewa na sanyaya-haɗi (murmurewa na jinkiri a cikin `getProviderCredentials` har yanzu yana aiki).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_REASONING_CACHE_CLEANUP_INTERVAL_MS` | `1800000`                                           | `src/lib/jobs/reasoningCacheCleanupJob.ts`                  | Matsayin lokacin tsabtace cache na hujja (ms). Mafi ƙanƙanta `60000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_REASONING_MIN_BUDGET`                | _ba a saita ba_ (an kashe)                          | `open-sse/services/reasoningTokenBuffer.ts`                 | Ƙananan ƙima ta zaɓi don kasafin fitarwa na samfuran tunani: `max_tokens` na mai kira a cikin `[256, floor)` ana ɗaga shi zuwa ƙananan ƙimar (wanda iyakar fitarwar samfurin ke takaita). Ba a saita ba = ba a taɗa ƙara girman kasafin abokin ciniki (#9507).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_REASONING_EFFORT_PROBE_PROVIDERS`    | _ba a saita ba_ (an kashe)                          | `open-sse/services/learnedReasoningEffortCaps.ts`           | Zaɓi, id na masu samarwa da aka raba da waƙafi (ko `*`): idan `reasoning_effort` 400/422 bai ambaci wani saitin da aka karɓa ba, sake gwadawa sau ɗaya da mataki ɗaya mafi ƙaranci (kada a taɗa ƙasa da `low`) kuma a koya wannan matakin ne kawai idan an amsa binciken (#14895).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOG_EXPORT_CRON`                     | `0 * * * *`                                         | `src/lib/jobs/logExportJob.ts`                              | Bayanin cron (UTC) don aikin fitar da rajistan kira wanda ke kwashe kowace manufar fitar da rajista da aka kunna. Fi amfani da `*/5 * * * *` idan ana fitar da jikoki.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOG_EXPORT_RUN_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `900000`                                            | `src/lib/logExport/childRunner.ts`                          | Ƙarar lokacin bango na kowace manufa ta yaro a milliseconds. Ƙarar lokaci tana yin rikodin gazawa kuma tana kashe aikin ba tare da ci gaba da ɗigon da ba a karɓa ba. Ƙimar marasa inganci/marasa tabbatacce/marasa cikakkun lambobi suna amfani da tsoho.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOG_EXPORT_CHILD_MAX_OLD_SPACE_MB`   | `1024`                                              | `src/lib/logExport/childRunner.ts`                          | Iyakar old-space na V8 na aikin fitarwa na Node a MB (ba jimlar RSS ba). Tsoho yana barin wuri don layuka 500 na kayayyakin da aka takaita tare da jikoki; ɗigon da suka yi girma suna iya ƙarewa da heap na yaro. Gazawa tana ba da shawarar rage girman ɗigo ko ɗaga wannan iyaka. Ƙimar marasa inganci/marasa tabbatacce/marasa cikakkun lambobi suna amfani da tsoho. Fitarwar Bun tana komawa cikin aikin ba tare da wannan iyaka ba.                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONFIG_HOT_RELOAD_MS`                | `5000`                                              | `src/lib/config/hotReload.ts`                               | Tazarar zana (ms) don sake loda saiti a zafi. Ana ƙin ƙasa da `1000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_REDIS_AUTH_CACHE`            | _(an kunna)_                                        | `src/lib/db/apiKeys.ts`                                     | Saita `1` don wuce cache na tabbatar da maɓallin API da Redis ke tallafawa (yana tilasta karanta DB).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_RTK_TRUST_PROJECT_FILTERS`           | `0`                                                 | `open-sse/services/compression/engines/rtk/filterLoader.ts` | Amince da dokokin tacewa na aikin RTK da mai amfani ke sarrafawa ba tare da tsauraran duban sa hannu ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_LITE_MAX_TOOL_LENGTH`                | `2000`                                              | `open-sse/services/compression/lite.ts`                     | Iyakar haruffa don guntata sakamakon kayan aiki na Lite idan ba a saita `lite.maxToolLength` ba. Kewayon 256–1000000. Saitin dashboard yana rinjayar wannan env.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNI_COMPRESSION_WORKERS`                      | `2`                                                 | `open-sse/services/compression/compressionWorkerPool.ts`    | Mafi girman ma'aikatan RTK/Caveman masu aiki a lokaci ɗaya; ƙarin ayyuka suna jira FIFO.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNI_COMPRESSION_WORKER_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `120000`                                            | `open-sse/services/compression/compressionWorkerPool.ts`    | Ƙarar lokaci na kowane aiki a milliseconds. Ana kawar da ma'aikatan da suka wuce lokaci kuma buƙatar tana kasa a buɗe ba tare da canji ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNI_COMPRESSION_WORKER_IDLE_MS`               | `60000`                                             | `open-sse/services/compression/compressionWorkerPool.ts`    | Rayuwar zaman banza a milliseconds kafin a kawar da ma'aikacin matsawa da ba a amfani da shi ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNI_COMPRESSION_WORKER_MAX_OLD_MB`            | `1024`                                              | `open-sse/services/compression/compressionWorkerPool.ts`    | Iyakar heap na V8 old-generation a MB ga kowane compression worker, a duk yanayoyi (mafi ƙaranci `128`). Aikin da ya wuce ita yana fail open (ba a matsa ba) maimakon ya ƙara girman heap na gateway.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `COMPRESSION_PIPELINE_BREAKER_ENABLED`          | `false`                                             | `open-sse/services/compression/pipelineEngineBreaker.ts`    | Babban switch na circuit-breaker na kowane engine don T02 stacked-pipeline. **Zaɓin shiga ne (tsoho a kashe)** — idan an kunna, engine da yake jefa kuskure akai-akai a fadin buƙatoci ana tsallake shi (fail-open) na tsawon cooldown; a kashe = halin gado daidai byte-da-byte.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `COMPRESSION_PIPELINE_BREAKER_THRESHOLD`        | `3`                                                 | `open-sse/services/compression/pipelineEngineBreaker.ts`    | Gazawori masu jere a fadin buƙatoci kafin breaker na engine ya buɗe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `COMPRESSION_PIPELINE_BREAKER_COOLDOWN_MS`      | `30000`                                             | `open-sse/services/compression/pipelineEngineBreaker.ts`    | Adadin milliseconds da engine da aka buɗe zai ci gaba da zama a tsallake kafin a yi half-open probe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `COMPRESSION_CCR_RETRIEVAL_RAMP_FACTOR`         | `2`                                                 | `open-sse/services/compression/engines/ccr/index.ts`        | T08/H8 CCR retrieval-feedback ramp: kowane retrieval na baya na block da aka adana yana ɗaga ingantaccen `minChars` a hanya ta linear (abun da ake retrieval akai-akai yana matsuwa ƙasa; `>=3` retrievals = ba a matsuwa ko kaɗan). `1` yana kashe ramp (binary skip a matakin kawai).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `COMPRESSION_CCR_DURABLE_STORE`                 | `true`                                              | `open-sse/services/compression/engines/ccr/index.ts`        | Mazabin block na CCR mai ɗorewa (#9061). Yana tallafa wa adanar in-memory da SQLite domin block ya tsira daga LRU eviction, TTL, restart, ko retrieve da ya sauka a wani instance. Saita `false` don riƙe blocks a cikin memory kawai. Blocks sama da 512KB da cloud runtimes suna memory kawai duk da haka.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `COMPRESSION_PREFIX_FREEZE_ENABLED`             | `false`                                             | `open-sse/services/compression/prefixFreeze.ts`             | T08/H5 babban switch na usage-observed prefix freeze. **Zaɓin shiga ne (tsoho a kashe)** — idan an kunna, system prompt da aka lura `>=` matakin ana ɗaukarsa azaman prefix mai kwanciyar hankali wanda za a iya cache kuma ana kiyaye shi daga matsuwa ko ma ga providers waɗanda static cache heuristic ba ta kama ba (freeze kawai yana _kiyayewa_, ba ya canza komai).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `COMPRESSION_PREFIX_FREEZE_THRESHOLD`           | `3`                                                 | `open-sse/services/compression/prefixFreeze.ts`             | Sau da yawa da aka lura da system prompt kafin a ɗauke shi azaman prefix mai daskarewa mai kwanciyar hankali.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_BOOTSTRAPPED`                        | `false`                                             | `src/app/(dashboard)/dashboard/page.tsx`                    | Bootstrap script yana saita `true` bayan saitin farko. Yana sarrafa ganuwar setup wizard.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_BODY_PROJECT_OVERRIDE`         | `0`                                                 | `open-sse/executors/antigravity.ts`                         | Hanyar kubucewa: bada damar request body ya override filin project na Antigravity.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_CREDITS`                           | `off`                                               | `open-sse/services/antigravityCredits.ts`                   | Manufar Google One AI credits: `off` ba ya saka credits ko kaɗan, `retry` yana saka sau ɗaya bayan quota 429 da ya cancanta, kuma `always` yana saka a buƙata ta farko.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_ALLOW_SIGNATURE_BYPASS`            | `0`                                                 | `open-sse/translator/request/openai-to-gemini.ts`           | Bada damar Antigravity request translator ya tsallake tantance request-signature na CLI mai tsanani idan upstream ya ƙi sahihiyar signatures (yanayin debug/antiquated-CLI). Duk wata ƙima marar sifili tana kunna bypass.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `AGY_TOKEN_FILE`                                | `~/.gemini/antigravity-cli/antigravity-oauth-token` | `src/app/api/providers/agy-auth/apply-local/route.ts`       | Override hanyar fayil ɗin token na Antigravity CLI (agy) don shigo da login na gida ta auto-detect.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |

### Gadar OAuth CLI (Na Ciki)

| Mai canji           | Tsoho              | Fayil ɗin tushe                 | Bayani                                              |
| ------------------- | ------------------ | ------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_SERVER`  | ganewa ta atomatik | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | URL na uwar garken don gadar auth ta CLI↔OmniRoute. |
| `OMNIROUTE_TOKEN`   | _(ba a saita ba)_  | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Token na auth don gadar CLI.                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_USER_ID` | `cli`              | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | ID na mai amfani don zaman gadar CLI.               |
| `SERVER_URL`        | _(ba a saita ba)_  | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Laƙabin gado na `OMNIROUTE_SERVER`.                 |
| `CLI_TOKEN`         | _(ba a saita ba)_  | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Laƙabin gado na `OMNIROUTE_TOKEN`.                  |
| `CLI_USER_ID`       | _(ba a saita ba)_  | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Laƙabin gado na `OMNIROUTE_USER_ID`.                |

## 11. Takardun Shaida na Mai Bayar da OAuth

Takardun shaida da aka gina a ciki don **ci gaban localhost**. Don tura ayyuka a nesa (remote deployments), yi rajistar naka a kofar masu haɓakawa ta kowane mai bayarwa.

| Variable                                    | Mai Bayarwa             | Bayanin Kula                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| ------------------------------------------- | ----------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `CLAUDE_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                    | Claude Code (Anthropic) | Abokin ciniki na jama'a — babu buƙatar sirri.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_REDIRECT_URI`                  | Claude Code             | Sake rubuta URI na jujjuyawa. Na asali: `https://platform.claude.com/oauth/code/callback`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `CODEX_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                     | Codex / OpenAI          | Abokin ciniki na jama'a.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `GEMINI_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                    | Gemini (Google)         | Yana buƙatar `_SECRET` da ya dace.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `GEMINI_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`                | Gemini (Google)         | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `KIMI_CODING_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`               | Kimi Coding (Moonshot)  | Abokin ciniki na jama'a.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `MUSE_CODE_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                 | Muse Code (Meta)        | Sake rubutawa na zaɓi don id na abokin ciniki na device-flow na Muse CLI na jama'a. Bar shi ba a saita ba don amfani da abokin ciniki na jama'a da aka haɗa a ciki.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`               | Antigravity (Google)    | Yana buƙatar `_SECRET` da ya dace.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`           | Antigravity (Google)    | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `GITHUB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                    | GitHub Copilot          | Abokin ciniki na jama'a.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `GHE_COPILOT_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`               | GHE Copilot             | Sake rubutawa na zaɓi don id na abokin ciniki na OAuth na GitHub Enterprise Copilot. Yana koma zuwa na asali na jama'a na `GITHUB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` idan ba a saita shi ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `COPILOT_INTEGRATION_ID`                    | GitHub Copilot          | Sake rubutawa na zaɓi don ID na haɗin kai na abokin ciniki na GitHub Copilot da ake aikawa a cikin kanun labarai na `Copilot-Integration-Id` da `Editor-Plugin-Version`. Na asali shine `copilot-developer-cli`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `WINDSURF_API_KEY`                          | Windsurf / Devin (v3.8) | Mabuɗin API na madadin da `open-sse/executors/devin-cli.ts` ke amfani da shi idan babu takardar shaida ta kowane haɗi. Na zaɓi ne.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `CLI_DEVIN_BIN`                             | Devin CLI (v3.8)        | Hanyar da aka keɓance zuwa binary na Devin CLI (`devin`). `open-sse/executors/devin-cli.ts` ne ke warwarewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `GITLAB_DUO_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                | GitLab Duo (v3.8)       | ID na abokin ciniki na OAuth don GitLab Duo. Yi rajistar app a `https://gitlab.com/-/profile/applications` tare da URI na jujjuyawa `<NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL>/callback` da scopes `api, read_user, openid, profile, email`. Yana koma zuwa `GITLAB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `GITLAB_DUO_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`            | GitLab Duo (v3.8)       | Sirrin abokin ciniki na OAuth don GitLab Duo. Na zaɓi ne — kwarara na PKCE ba ya buƙatar sirri. Yana koma zuwa `GITLAB_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
