Yana koma zuwa `GITLAB_BASE_URL`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `GITLAB_BASE_URL`                           | GitLab Duo (v3.8)       | Madadin tsoho na `GITLAB_DUO_BASE_URL`. Ana amfani da shi idan ba a saita bambancewar `_DUO_` ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `GITLAB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                    | GitLab Duo (v3.8)       | Madadin tsoho na `GITLAB_DUO_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` wanda `src/lib/oauth/constants/oauth.ts` ke amfani da shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `GITLAB_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`                | GitLab Duo (v3.8)       | Madadin tsoho na `GITLAB_DUO_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET` wanda `src/lib/oauth/constants/oauth.ts` ke amfani da shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `QODER_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`                 | Qoder                   | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `QODER_OAUTH_AUTHORIZE_URL`                 | Qoder                   | Saita shi don kunna Qoder OAuth.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `QODER_OAUTH_TOKEN_URL`                     | Qoder                   | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `QODER_OAUTH_USERINFO_URL`                  | Qoder                   | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `QODER_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                     | Qoder                   | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `QODER_PERSONAL_ACCESS_TOKEN`               | Qoder                   | Madadin maɓallin API kai tsaye (yana wuce OAuth).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `QODER_CLI_WORKSPACE`                       | Qoder                   | ID na workspace don Qoder CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_QODER_WORKSPACE`                 | Qoder                   | Laqabin `QODER_CLI_WORKSPACE`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `QODER_CLI_CONFIG_DIR`                      | Qoder                   | Sake rubuta kundin config na Qoder CLI (zama na PAT da aka ware, yana guje wa lalata shiga browser).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `QODER_CLI_MAX_CONCURRENCY`                 | Qoder                   | Mafi girman gudanarwar qodercli a lokaci guda (print spawns ko ACP prompts); ƙarin buƙatun suna jerawa FIFO. Ba a saita ba (tsoho) = babu iyaka.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `QODER_CLI_QUEUE_TIMEOUT_MS`                | Qoder                   | Tsawon lokacin da buƙatar Qoder ke jiran wurin qodercli (kawai idan an saita iyaka) kafin ta gaza da 429 `qodercli_busy` + `Retry-After`. Tsoho `120000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `QODER_CLI_TRANSPORT`                       | Qoder                   | `print` (tsoho) yana ƙaddamar da `qodercli --print` ga kowace buƙata; `acp` yana riƙe tsari ɗaya mai dawwama na `qodercli --acp` ga kowane PAT.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `QODER_CLI_ACP_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS`             | Qoder                   | Sufurin ACP: kashe tsarin qodercli mai dawwama bayan wannan lokacin rashin aiki. Tsoho `600000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `QODER_CLI_ACP_STARTUP_TIMEOUT_MS`          | Qoder                   | Sufurin ACP: wa'adin ƙarshe da qodercli zai amsa ACP `initialize`. Tsoho `60000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `QODER_CLI_ACP_MAX_SESSIONS_PER_PROCESS`    | Qoder                   | Sufurin ACP: sake juyawar tsarin qodercli mai dawwama bayan wannan adadin zamani. Tsoho `500`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `BLACKBOX_WEB_VALIDATED_TOKEN`              | Blackbox Web            | Token `tk` na frontend da za a aika a matsayin `validated` a kan `/api/chat`. Ana buƙatansa idan Blackbox yana tilasta daidaita token; in ba haka ba, OmniRoute yana koma zuwa UUID na bazuwar. Duba batu #2252.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `VISION_BRIDGE_BASE_URL`                    | Vision Bridge guardrail | URL na tushe mai jituwa da OpenAI don kiran vision-bridge waɗanda ba na Anthropic ba ne. Ya dawo da tsoho zuwa tsohuwar URL env ta OpenAI ko api.openai.com. Yi nuni zuwa `/v1` self-loop na OmniRoute ko kowace ƙarshen hanya (endpoint) mai jituwa da OpenAI (Gemini OpenAI-compat, OpenRouter). Issue #2232. Idan URL ɗin shine `/v1` na OmniRoute kanta, ƙaramar buƙatar describe tana aika `x-omniroute-admission-bypass: internal` kuma tana tantancewa da shaidar self-loop da aka warware (`sk_omniroute` sentinel a yanayin local, ko `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY` / `ROUTER_API_KEY` — #1350) domin deployments na `REQUIRE_API_KEY=true` su yi aiki. |
| `VISION_BRIDGE_API_KEY`                     | Vision Bridge guardrail | Mabuɗin API don URL ɗin da ke sama. Yana maye gurbin env vars na OpenAI / Google na kowace mai samarwa don kiran vision-bridge waɗanda ba na Anthropic ba ne. Samfuran Anthropic suna ci gaba da amfani da hanyar mabuɗin Anthropic na musamman. Issue #2232.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_VISION_BRIDGE_NEGATIVE_CACHE_MS` | Vision Bridge guardrail | Tsawon lokacin da za a adana sakamakon jigilar "babu ɗan takara da za a iya amfani da shi" a cache, a ms (tsoho `30000`). Ƙima mara inganci ko mara kyau tana komawa zuwa tsohon; `0` yana kashe negative cache ɗin. Tushe: `src/lib/guardrails/visionBridgeRouter.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |

> [!WARNING]
>
> 1. Je zuwa [Google Cloud Console → Credentials](https://console.cloud.google.com/apis/credentials)
> 2. Ƙirƙiri OAuth 2.0 Client ID (nau'i: "Web application")
> 3. Ƙara URL na umarwar ka a matsayin Authorized redirect URI
> 4. Maye gurbin ƙimomin takardun shaida a cikin `.env`.

---

## 12. Sauya User-Agent na Masu Bayar da Sabis

Sauya kanun `User-Agent` da ake aikawa zuwa ga kowane mai bayar da sabis na sama. Ana warware wannan ta hanya mai canzawa a lokacin gudanarwa ta hanyar ajin tushe na mai aiwatarwa:

```
process.env[`${PROVIDER_ID}_USER_AGENT`]
```

> **Tushe:** `open-sse/executors/base.ts` → `buildHeaders()`

| Mai Canji | Darajar Tsoho | Yaushe Ake Sabuntawa |
| -------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CLAUDE_USER_AGENT` | `claude-cli/2.1.280 (external, cli)` | Lokacin da Anthropic ta fitar da sabon sigar CLI |
| `CLAUDE_DISABLE_TOOL_NAME_CLOAK` | `false` | `executors/base.ts` + `executors/cliproxyapi.ts` | Saita zuwa `1`/`true` don aika sunayen kayan aikin harness na ɓangare na uku kalma-da-kalma zuwa Anthropic a dukkan hanyoyin da ke zuwa Anthropic (native OAuth da CLIProxyAPI). A tsoho, mai aiwatarwa yana ba da sunayen barkwanci a hanya tabbatacciya ga sunayen kayan aikin da ba na Claude Code ba (Claude Code canonical mapping inda akwai, in ba haka ba PascalCase) kuma yana mayar da su a kan amsar ta `_toolNameMap`, don haka ba a ƙin harnesses masu kayan aikin snake_case azaman abokan ciniki na ɓangare na uku da aka gano alamomin su. Domin gano matsaloli kawai. |
| `CODEX_USER_AGENT` | `codex-cli/0.155.0 (Windows 10.0.26200; x64)` | Lokacin da OpenAI ta sabunta Codex CLI |
| `CODEX_CLIENT_VERSION` | `0.155.0` | Sauya sigar abokin ciniki na Codex kai tsaye ba tare da cikakken jerin UA ba |
| `GROK_CLI_CLIENT_VERSION` | `1.0.41` | Sauya `x-grok-client-version` don inference, daidaita samfuri, da OAuth na lambar na'ura, tare da tsohon User-Agent na Grok CLI don inference da daidaita samfuri. Yana karɓar SemVer tare da zaɓin metadata na prerelease/build, har zuwa haruffa 128 bayan an cire sararin da ke kewaye. Ƙimomin da ba a saita ba, fanko, marasa inganci, ko masu ɗauke da karya layi suna amfani da pin da aka aika. Sauya cikakken User-Agent na inference da ke akwai yana ci gaba da fifiko. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_CLIENT_VERSION` | `2.1.280` | Sauya sigar Claude Code da aka sanar kai tsaye ba tare da `CLAUDE_USER_AGENT` ba. Anthropic na takaita wasu samfuri bisa wannan ƙima (#12417). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_CLIENT_BUILD_REVISION` | `1e2` | Sauya haɗin ƙarshe na haruffa 3 da OmniRoute ke haɗawa zuwa `cc_version=` a cikin toshe na billing na Claude. Ƙara shi tare da `CLAUDE_CODE_CLIENT_VERSION` — kafkafa siga kawai yana sanar da ma'aurata `version.revision` da babu ainihin binary da ke fitar da ita. |
| `GITHUB_COPILOT_CLI_VERSION` | `1.0.91` | Sauya sigar Copilot CLI da aka sanar kai tsaye ba tare da `GITHUB_USER_AGENT` ba |
| `GITHUB_USER_AGENT` | `GitHubCopilotChat/0.54.0` | Lokacin da GitHub Copilot Chat ya sabunta |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_USER_AGENT` | `antigravity/2.0.1 darwin/arm64` | Lokacin da Antigravity IDE ya sabunta |
| `KIRO_USER_AGENT` | `AWS-SDK-JS/3.0.0 kiro-ide/1.0.0` | Lokacin da Kiro IDE ya sabunta |
| `KIRO_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` | `kiro-cli` | Sauya `clientId` na zamantakewa na lambar na'ura ta Kiro (id na jama'a) |
| `KIRO_VERIFY_FULL_CRC` | `false` | Ta zaɓi: cikakken tantance CRC na saƙon kowace frame a kan kwararar abubuwan Kiro (gwada kwararar da suka lalace) |
| `KIRO_CREDIT_PRICE_USD` | `0.02` | USD ga kowace bashi na Kiro; yana mayar da bashin `meteringEvent` zuwa ainihin kuɗin kowace buƙata |
| `KIRO_REQUIRE_CREDITS` | `false` | Kashe amsar Kiro da ba ta ba da rahoton auna bashi ba maimakon rubuta ta ba tare da lissafa kuɗi ba |
| `QODER_USER_AGENT` | `Qoder-Cli` | Lokacin da Qoder CLI ya sabunta |
| `CURSOR_USER_AGENT` | `Cursor/3.3` | Lokacin da Cursor ya sabunta |

> [!TIP]
> Kuna iya ƙara sauya User-Agent ga **kowane** mai bayar da sabis ta amfani da tsarin `{PROVIDER_ID}_USER_AGENT`. Mai aiwatarwa yana gina sunan canjin muhalli ta hanya mai canzawa.

---

## 13. Daidaituwar Sawun Yatsa na CLI

Idan aka kunna, OmniRoute yana sake tsara taken HTTP da filayen jikin JSON domin su dace da ainihin sa hannun kayan aikin CLI na hukuma. Wannan yana rage haɗarin yi wa asusu alama, tare da kiyaye IP na proxy ɗinka.

**Tushe:** `open-sse/config/cliFingerprints.ts`, `open-sse/executors/base.ts`

### Na Kowane Mai Bayarwa

| Sauyi                    | Kunnawa | Tasiri                                           |
| ------------------------ | ------- | ------------------------------------------------ |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CODEX`       | `=1`    | Yana kwaikwayon sa hannun buƙatar Codex CLI      |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CLAUDE`      | `=1`    | Yana kwaikwayon sa hannun buƙatar Claude Code    |
| `CLI_COMPAT_GITHUB`      | `=1`    | Yana kwaikwayon sa hannun buƙatar GitHub Copilot |
| `CLI_COMPAT_ANTIGRAVITY` | `=1`    | Yana kwaikwayon sa hannun buƙatar Antigravity    |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CURSOR`      | `=1`    | Yana kwaikwayon sa hannun buƙatar Cursor         |
| `CLI_COMPAT_KIMI_CODING` | `=1`    | Yana kwaikwayon sa hannun buƙatar Kimi Coding    |
| `CLI_COMPAT_KILOCODE`    | `=1`    | Yana kwaikwayon sa hannun buƙatar Kilo Code      |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CLINE`       | `=1`    | Yana kwaikwayon sa hannun buƙatar Cline          |

### Na Gabaɗaya

| Sauyi            | Kunnawa | Tasiri                                                             |
| ---------------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `CLI_COMPAT_ALL` | `=1`    | Kunna daidaituwar sawun yatsa ga **duk** masu bayarwa lokaci guda. |

### Sauya bayanan asalin Kimi Coding CLI

| Sauyi                   | Tsoho                   | Fayil ɗin Tushe                          | Bayani                                                                                |
| ----------------------- | ----------------------- | ---------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `KIMI_CLI_VERSION`      | `1.36.0`                | `src/lib/oauth/providers/kimi-coding.ts` | Sauya sigar Kimi CLI da ake aikawa yayin kiran OAuth/API.                             |
| `KIMI_CODING_DEVICE_ID` | _(tsohon da aka ɗauka)_ | `src/lib/oauth/providers/kimi-coding.ts` | Sauya ID na na'urar Kimi da aka ɗauka, wanda ake amfani da shi a taken abokin ciniki. |

> [!NOTE]
> Wannan fasalin yana aiki tare da sauye-sauyen User-Agent (§12). Tsarin sawun yatsa yana kula da jerin taken da jerin filayen jiki, yayin da sauye-sauyen User-Agent ke kula da takamaiman zaren UA. Ana iya kunna kowannensu da kansa.

---

## 14. Masu Bayar da Maɓallin API

Maɓallan API na masu bayarwa waɗanda ke amfani da tantancewa kai tsaye. **Saitin da aka fi so:** Dashboard → Providers → Add API Key.

Saita su ta hanyar sauye-sauyen muhalli wata hanya ce ta daban don girkawar Docker ko tsarin da ba shi da dubawar allo.

Tsarin da ake ganewa: `{PROVIDER_ID}_API_KEY`

| Sauyi              | Mai Bayarwa                                         |
| ------------------ | --------------------------------------------------- |
| `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY` | DeepSeek                                            |
| `NVIDIA_API_KEY`   | NVIDIA NIM                                          |
| `JINA_AI_API_KEY`  | Jina AI (Foundation API + madadin Reader)           |
| `JINA_API_KEY`     | Jina AI (laƙabi na `JINA_AI_API_KEY`)               |
| `GEMINI_API_KEY`   | Gemini (Google AI Studio) embeddings + madadin taɗi |
| `GOOGLE_API_KEY`   | Gemini (laƙabi na `GEMINI_API_KEY`)                 |

> [!NOTE]
> An cire shigarwar `${PROVIDER}_API_KEY` na dindindin don Groq, xAI, Mistral, Perplexity, Together AI, Fireworks, Cerebras, Cohere, Nebius, da Qianfan a v3.8.0 saboda runtime ba ya ƙara karanta su — waɗannan masu bayarwa suna dogara ne kawai da Dashboard / `data/provider-credentials.json` / DB mai ɓoyewa. Duba sashen _Bincike: Sauye-sauyen da Aka Cire / Marasa Amfani_ a ƙasan wannan takarda don hanyar ƙaura.

> [!TIP]
> Maɓallan da aka saita ta Dashboard ana adana su da ɓoyewa a SQLite kuma suna da fifiko kan sauye-sauyen muhalli.
>
> **Jina:** embeddings na `jina-ai/…`, rerank, classify, segment, da `jina-search` ba sa cajin maɓallin muhallin cluster idan akwai haɗin dashboard na `jina-ai` (ko `jina-reader` na bai ɗaya) — `getProviderCredentials` yana cike na farko. Ana amfani da `JINA_AI_API_KEY` / `JINA_API_KEY` ne kawai idan babu maɓallin dashboard mai amfani. Rajistan kira yana danganta madadin muhalli da `connection_id=env:JINA_AI_API_KEY`. Katin Reader (`jina-reader`, `r.jina.ai`) ba ya taɓa ba da `/v1/embeddings` ko `/v1/rerank`.
>
> **Gemini:** `gemini/gemini-embedding-2` (laƙabi `google/gemini-embedding-2`) yana fara amfani da haɗin `gemini` na dashboard. Ana amfani da `GEMINI_API_KEY` / `GOOGLE_API_KEY` ne kawai idan babu maɓallin dashboard mai amfani. Rajistan kira yana danganta madadin muhalli da `connection_id=env:GEMINI_API_KEY`. Zirga-zirgar multimodal ta asali tana amfani da `x-goog-api-key` zuwa `:embedContent` / `:batchEmbedContents` — abubuwan OpenAI `input` guda N suna zama vectors guda N.

---

## 15. Saitunan Ƙarewar Lokaci (Timeout)

Duk ƙimar suna cikin **milliseconds**. Ana warware su a tsakiya a cikin `src/shared/utils/runtimeTimeouts.ts`.

### Tsarin Matakala na Timeout

```
REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS (global override)
├─→ FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS (upstream provider calls, default: 600000)
│   ├─→ FETCH_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS (inherits from FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS)
│   ├─→ FETCH_BODY_TIMEOUT_MS (inherits from FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS)
│   ├─→ TLS_CLIENT_TIMEOUT_MS (inherits from FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS)
│   │   └── TLS_FIRST_BYTE_WATCHDOG_MS (independent, default: 10000)
│   ├── RESPONSES_FIRST_BYTE_TIMEOUT_MS (independent, default: 15000)
│   ├── OPENCODE_RESPONSES_HEADERS_WAIT_MS (independent, default: 0 = off, suggested: 30000)
│   ├── OPENCODE_RESPONSES_HEADERS_WAIT_MAX_ROTATIONS (independent, default: 2)
│   ├── FETCH_CONNECT_TIMEOUT_MS (independent, default: 30000)
│   └── FETCH_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS (independent, default: 4000)
├─→ STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS (inherits from REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS, default: 600000)
├─→ STREAM_ACTIVE_TIMEOUT_MS (independent, default: 1260000; 0 disables)
├─→ STREAM_READINESS_TIMEOUT_MS (inherits from REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS, default: 80000)
├─→ STREAM_READINESS_MAX_TIMEOUT_MS (caps adaptive readiness extensions, default: 180000)
└─→ API_BRIDGE_PROXY_TIMEOUT_MS (inherits from REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS, default: 30000)
    ├─→ API_BRIDGE_SERVER_REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS (derived, default: 300000)
    ├── API_BRIDGE_SERVER_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS (default: 60000)
    ├── API_BRIDGE_SERVER_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS (default: 5000)
    └── API_BRIDGE_SERVER_SOCKET_TIMEOUT_MS (default: 0 = disabled)
```

| Mai canji                                       | Tsoho                                                             | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| ----------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS`                            | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                 | Gajeren hanya ta duniya — tana maye gurbin tsoffin dabi'u na duka `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS` da `STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                              | `600000`                                                          | Jimillar ƙarewar lokacin buƙatar HTTP don kiran masu samarwa na upstream.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS`                        | `600000`                                                          | Mafi yawan shiru tsakanin bytes na upstream kafin a fice. Samfuran tunani mai tsawo ba su kasafai yin hutu fiye da >90s ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `STREAM_ACTIVE_TIMEOUT_MS`                      | `1260000`                                                         | Mafi yawan rayuwar rafanar SSE mai aiki gaba ɗaya; ba ya sake farawa a kan bytes na upstream kuma yana zaman kansa daga `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS`. An samo shi daga mafi girman `timeoutMs` na kowane samfuri a cikin rajista (1200000, Codex) tare da ƙarin 60000, don haka ba a taɓa kashe samfuri da aka ba shi izinin amfani da cikakken kasafinsa a tsakiyar amsa. Saita shi zuwa `0` don kashewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_SSE_COMMENTS`                        | _(an kashe)_                                                      | Ko OmniRoute zai iya fitar da layukan sharhi na SSE `:` (misali bugun rayuwa `: keepalive` da trailers na metadata `x-omniroute-*`). An kashe shi a matsayin tsoho (#10524) tunda abokan ciniki masu tsananin dacewa da OpenAI suna JSON.parse kowace layin SSE kuma suna karyewa a kan sharhin `:`; ba a shafar heartbeats na `data:`. Saita `on`/`true`/`1`/`yes` don sake shiga. Ana amfani da shi a `open-sse/utils/sseHeartbeat.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `SSE_COALESCE_MS`                               | `0` (a kashe)                                                     | Zaɓi na son rai: haɗa abubuwan `content_block_delta` mafari na Claude (masu wannan index na toshe da nau'in delta) da ake aikawa ga abokan cinikin Claude, tare da fitarwa akalla kowane N ms. Babu asara; ba a taɓa haɗa `signature_delta` da abubuwan iyaka. Yana shafar kowane nau'in delta sai idan an saita mai canji na kowane nau'i a ƙasa. Ana amfani da shi a `open-sse/utils/sseDeltaCoalesce.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `SSE_COALESCE_THINKING_MS`                      | `0` (a kashe)                                                     | Maye gurbi na kowane nau'i na `SSE_COALESCE_MS` don `thinking_delta`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `SSE_COALESCE_TEXT_MS`                          | `0` (a kashe)                                                     | Maye gurbi na kowane nau'i na `SSE_COALESCE_MS` don `text_delta`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `SSE_COALESCE_INPUT_JSON_MS`                    | `0` (a kashe)                                                     | Maye gurbi na kowane nau'i na `SSE_COALESCE_MS` don `input_json_delta`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `SSE_FRAME_COALESCE_MS`                         | `0` (a kashe)                                                     | Zaɓi na son rai: tattara frames na SSE da aka aika a cikin N ms zuwa rubutu guda ɗaya zuwa ga abokin ciniki. Ba a taɓa jinkirta frame na farko da frames na ƙarshe (`[DONE]`, `message_stop`, `error`, `response.completed`/`failed`/`incomplete`); idan an kunna tattarawa, ITL yana auna gibin tattarawa. Ana amfani da shi a `open-sse/utils/sseOutputSink.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `SSE_STALL_WATCHDOG`                            | `timer`                                                           | Aiwatar da watchdog na tsayin bytes don amsoshi masu rafana. `timer` yana sake saita timer guda ɗaya ga kowane yanki na upstream (yana faɗowa daidai a kasafin tsayi); `interval` yana kiyaye bincike guda ɗaya kowace rafana da lokacin `clamp(budget/4, 1s, 5s)`, don haka tsayi yana faɗowa a cikin kasafin + lokaci. Ana amfani da shi a `open-sse/utils/streamStallWatchdog.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `STREAM_READINESS_TIMEOUT_MS`                   | `80000`                                                           | Lokacin karɓar farkon abin SSE wanda ba ping ba. Yana gāda `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS` idan an saita shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `STREAM_READINESS_MAX_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `180000`                                                          | Mafi girman tagar shirin farkon abu mai dacewa don buƙatun rafana masu girma, masu yawan kayan aiki, ko masu babban tunani.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_AGENT_GOAL_POLICY_ENABLED`           | `true`                                                            | Maɓallin kashewa don dabarar kiyasin `/goal`. Saita `false`/`0`/`off` don kashe gano gaba ɗaya — ba a taɓa ɗaukaka ƙarewar lokacin shiri da farfadowar rafana ta hanyar jiki/headers na buƙata, wanda ke rage ƙarin ƙarewar lokacin da abokin ciniki ke sarrafawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_AGENT_GOAL_READINESS_MAX_TIMEOUT_MS` | `600000`                                                          | Mafi girman tagar shirin farkon abu don gudun ayyukan `/goal` da aka gano ko buƙatun da aka tilasta da `x-omniroute-agent-goal`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_AGENT_GOAL_STREAM_RECOVERY`          | `true`                                                            | Kunna farfadowar rafana da wuri ta atomatik don gudun ayyukan `/goal` da aka gano. Saita `false`/`0`/`off` don kashe shiga ta musamman ga manufa. Wannan na iya KARA farfadowa kawai bisa tsohon dabi'ar mai sarrafawa — ba ya taɓa maye gurbin fita daga zaɓi na `STREAM_RECOVERY_ENABLED`/saitunan DB da aka bayyana.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_DROP_NONSTANDARD_EVENTS`       | `true`                                                            | Cire abubuwan SSE na `codex.*` marasa daidaituwa (misali `codex.rate_limits`) waɗanda ke karya `responses.stream()` na OpenAI SDK da 502. A KUNNE a matsayin tsoho (#11014). Saita `0`/`false`/`no`/`off` don aika su gaba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS`                  | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                 | Sufurin Codex app-server na zaɓi na son rai. Ƙarshen WebSocket (`ws://`/`wss://`) na sidecar `codex app-server` na gida. Lokacin da aka saita shi tare da alama, ana tura buƙatun Codex ta JSON-RPC zuwa sidecar maimakon HTTP Responses API. Ana iya saita shi kuma ga kowace haɗi ta `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerUrl`. Ana amfani da shi a `open-sse/executors/codex/appServerConfig.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS_TOKEN`            | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                 | Alamar iko/bearer a layi da ake gabatarwa wa app-server. Maye gurbi na kowace haɗi: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerToken`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS_TOKEN_FILE`       | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                 | Hanyar zuwa fayil da ke riƙe da token na iko na app-server (daga `codex app-server --ws-token-file`). Ana amfani da shi idan ba a saita `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS_TOKEN` ba. Sauyawa na kowane haɗi: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerTokenFile`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_CWD`                 | `/tmp`                                                            | Kundin aiki da juyin app-server yake gudana a ciki. Sauyawa na kowane haɗi: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerCwd`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_APPROVAL`            | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                 | Manufar amincewa da ake miƙa wa juyin app-server (misali `never`, `on-request`). Sauyawa na kowane haɗi: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerApprovalPolicy`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_SANDBOX`             | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                 | Manufar sandbox da ake miƙa wa juyin app-server (misali `read-only`, `workspace-write`, `danger-full-access`). Idan ba a saita ba, mai aiwatarwa yana komawa tsoho `workspace-write` (an ƙarfafa; a baya `danger-full-access`). Sauyawa na kowane haɗi: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerSandbox`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_AUTO_APPROVE`        | `false`                                                           | Amince kai tsaye da buƙatun amincewa na app-server da kansa (gudanar da umarni/fayil/izini a kan mai masauki). A kashe da tsoho — ana ƙin buƙatun kai tsaye; ba a shafar kiran kayan aikin harness (sun tafi ta hanyar wucewa `item/tool/call` dabam). Yana karɓar `true`/`1`/`yes`. Sauyawa na kowane haɗi: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerAutoApprove`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `FETCH_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`                      | = `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                                              | Lokacin karɓar kanun amsa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_FETCH_START_TIMEOUT_CAP_MS`          | `110000`                                                          | Mafi yawan jiran kanun amsa a kan buƙatun yawo (ms). `0` yana kashe wannan iyaka; `fetchStartTimeoutCapMs` na mai samarwa da aka bayyana shi ke da fifiko.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `30000` (30s)                                                     | Mafi yawan jiran fara amsa (ms) ga kowace ƙoƙari kai tsaye marar wakili. Cikar lokaci tana sake gwadawa sau ɗaya a kan socket sabo; saita `0` don kashe iyakar kuma riƙe halin da yake a baya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOCAL_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`     | `300000` (minti 5)                                                | Mafi ƙarancin fara amsa (ms) ga ƙoƙare-ƙoƙare kai tsaye marar wakili waɗanda mai masauki da aka nufa shi ne na sirri/LAN/loopback (misali `localhost`, `host.docker.internal`, `192.168.x.x`), domin kada tsoho na 30s da ke sama ya yanke fara Ollama na asali a sanyi. Yana aiki azaman mafi ƙaranci: jiran da yake da tasiri shi ne mafi girma daga cikin wannan ƙima da `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`; `0` ko ƙima mara inganci tana cire mafi ƙarancin gida. Ba ya shafar masu masauki na nesa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_RESPONSE_RETRY_TIMEOUT_MS`    | `600000` (minti 10)                                               | Bene (ms) ga ƙoƙarin SAKE GWADAWA na sabon socket bayan cikar lokacin fara amsa na ƙoƙarin da aka haɗa a sama (#13703). Yana aiki kawai idan mai kira ya riga ya haɗa sigina na wa'adinsa (jeren haɗi/samfur/mai samarwa/`FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS` da aka warware); wannan sigina ita ce ainihin iyakar kuma tana faɗuwa da farko a hanyar da aka yi niyya, don haka wannan tallafi ne mai zurfi ba iyaka madaidaiciya ba — ba tare da ita ba, sake gwadawar za ta sake amfani da wannan gajeren taga `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS` guda kamar ƙoƙarin da aka haɗa kuma ta yi 504 ga samfuran hujja masu lafiya da suke da TTFB mai jinkiri. Ba a taɗa barin ta kasa mafi ƙarancin da ke sama; idan mai kira bai ba da wani sigina na wa'adi ba gaba ɗaya, sake gwadawar tana ci gaba da riƙe mafi ƙarancin ba tare da canji ba. |
| `FETCH_BODY_TIMEOUT_MS`                         | = `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                                              | Lokacin karɓar cikakken jikin amsa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `FETCH_CONNECT_TIMEOUT_MS`                      | `30000`                                                           | Cikar lokacin kafa haɗin TCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `FETCH_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS`                    | `4000`                                                            | Cikar lokacin zaman banza na socket keep-alive.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `TLS_CLIENT_TIMEOUT_MS`                         | = `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                                              | Cikar lokacin wakilin TLS fingerprint (wreq-js).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `TLS_FIRST_BYTE_WATCHDOG_MS`                    | `10000`                                                           | Yana iyakance lokacin-zuwa-baiti-na-farko musamman a kan jikin sufuri na TLS-fingerprint na wreq-js; `TLS_CLIENT_TIMEOUT_MS` kaɗai ba ya iya kama jikin da ya tsaya tunda yana waruwa da zarar kanun sun isa (#12656). Cikar lokaci tana soke mai karatu na wreq kuma ta koma ga mai rarraba kai tsaye/wakili; `0` yana kashe mai sa ido.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `RESPONSES_FIRST_BYTE_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `15000`                                                           | Mai aiwatar da OpenCode kawai, kuma kawai sa'ad da alamar fasali `OPENCODE_RESPONSES_STALL_ROTATION` take kunne (tsoho a kashe): yana iyakance jiran baiti na farko na jiki na amsar Responses mai yawo bayan kanunta (#13484). Rigar Responses tana buɗewa da `response.created`, don haka shiru bayan wannan taga tsayawa ce: ana sanyaya asusun kuma buƙatar tana juya zuwa asusu na gaba sau ɗaya; tsayawa ta biyu tana gaza da sauri. `0` yana kashe gadin ko da alama a kunne take.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OPENCODE_RESPONSES_HEADERS_WAIT_MS`            | `0` (= a kashe)                                                   | Mai aiwatar da OpenCode kawai: yana iyakance jiran kanun amsar sama a kan kiran Responses mai yawo idan wani asusu yana nan — buƙatar da aka sa a jeri tana daina jira kuma ta ƙaura maimakon ta riƙe cikakken tagar kanun. Kawai kiran Responses masu yawo ne suke shafuwa (rigar Responses tana buɗewa da `response.created`, don haka shiru a can jeri ne, ba samarwa ba); kammala hira da kiran marasa yawo ba a taɗa su ba, kuma tagar tana gajerawa kawai ga benen fetch-start mai tasiri, ba ta taɗa shi ba. Ƙimar da aka shawarta lokacin kunna: `30000`. `0` yana kashe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OPENCODE_RESPONSES_HEADERS_WAIT_MAX_ROTATIONS` | `2`                                                               | OpenCode executor kawai: sau nawa a cikin kowace buƙata iyakar jiran-headers da ke sama na iya matsawa zuwa asusu na gaba. Asusu na ƙarshe da ya rage koyaushe yana riƙe cikakken tagar headers.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OPENCODE_PARK_AND_RESUME`                      | `false`                                                           | OpenCode executor kawai: ajiye buƙatar tare da heartbeat bayan maimaita 429 na wucin gadi (ko sabon alamar pool-strain), sannan sake kunna ƙafa guda mai iyaka har zuwa asusu 3 mataki-mataki maimakon rarraba dukkan jirgin (#13924). A kashe tazo da farko: kowace 429 tana jujjuya zuwa asusu na gaba kamar yadda yake a baya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OPENCODE_PARK_REPLAY_SERVE_SETASIDE`           | `false`                                                           | OpenCode executor kawai: idan kowane asusu da ya shirya cooldown a cikin ƙafar replay ta park-and-resume an ajiye shi gefe ta ƙwaƙwalwar ƙin-proxy, a yi musu hidima duk da haka maimakon kada a sake kunna kome (#14851, ci gaba na #14750). A kashe tazo da farko: ƙafar replay tana fanko a wannan yanayin, kamar kafin #14750.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `STREAM_READINESS_STALL_RETRY`                  | `false`                                                           | Streaming chat kawai: idan jikin upstream na farko ya tsaya kafin ya samar da abin da zai yi amfani, a fitar da ƙoƙari na biyu ɗaya mai iyaka ta hanyar tsarawa ɗaya tare da kasafin readiness ɗaya kuma babu hukunci ga asusu. A kashe tazo da farko: jiki na farko da ya tsaya yana sa buƙatar ta gaza ba tare da sake gwadawa ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OPENCODE_POOL_RESELECT`                        | `false`                                                           | OpenCode executor kawai: bayan 429 daga mai bayarwa da aka rarrabe ta egress a kan asusu mara proxy a ƙarƙashin mahallin pool na muhalli, a tambayi connection pool wani memba don ƙoƙari na gaba maimakon sake gwada adireshin egress ɗaya. Yana tsarawa ne kawai, bai fitar da kowa ba: pool da ya ƙare yana ci gaba da halin yanzu. A kashe tazo da farko: kowace 429 tana jujjuya zuwa asusu na gaba kamar yadda yake a baya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OPENCODE_POOL_STRAIN_MARKER_PATH`              | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                 | OpenCode executor kawai: hanyar maye gurbin alamar pool-strain da ake karantawa kafin ajiyewa (`{since, reason, ttl_s}`, tazo da farko `/tmp/opencode-pool-strain.json`, #13924). Sabuwar alama tana ajiyewa ba tare da sake ƙidayawa ba; idan ba ta nan ko ta ɓace tana komawa zuwa ƙidayan burst.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

| `OMNIROUTE_NOTION_TLS_GRACE_MS`                 | `10000`                                                           | Ƙarin lokacin jinkiri na ƙarshe (hard-deadline grace) na JS wanda aka ƙara a kan timeout na asali.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |

| `OBSCURA_CDP_ENDPOINT`                          | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                 | Yi nuni ga Obscura da yake gudana riga (`http://host:port`) maimakon ƙaddamar da sabo; module baya mallakar wannan process (`open-sse/services/obscura.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OBSCURA_PORT`                                  | `random free port`                                                | Port da aka ƙayyade domin `obscura serve` da aka ƙaddamar; ana zaɓar port mai kyau ta atomatik idan ba a saita ba (`open-sse/services/obscura.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `WEB_COOKIE_USE_BROWSER`                        | `0`                                                               | Shigar da buƙatar tattaunawar web-cookie cikin hanyar da browser ke tallafawa (`browserBackedChat.ts`); `1` don kunna.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `KIMI_WEB_BASE_URL`                             | `https://www.kimi.ai`                                             | URL na tushe domin executor na Kimi Web (kimi.ai Connect-RPC na ƙasashen waje) (`kimi-web.ts`); yi override kawai domin mirror/proxy endpoints.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `KIMI_WEB_CHAT_URL`                             | `<KIMI_WEB_BASE_URL>/apiv2/kimi.gateway.chat.v1.ChatService/Chat` | Cikakken chat endpoint domin executor na Kimi Web (`kimi-web.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOGIN_BROWSER_PATH`                  | _(ana gano shi ta atomatik)_                                      | Hanyar zuwa executable na Chrome/Edge na tsarin domin shiga browser mai hulɗa na Adobe Firefly (`adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts`); yana override gano ta atomatik na kowane OS.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_STANDALONE_DIR`                      | _fitarwar standalone ta .build/_                                  | Override na lokacin gini domin directory na fitarwar standalone wanda matakin colocation na bayan gini ke amfani da shi (`scripts/build/colocate-standalone.mjs`); kayan aikin gini ne, ba runtime ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

Ƙoƙarin target na combo suna gādar timeout na buƙatun upstream da aka warware (`FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`, ko
`REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS` idan shi ne yake ba da tsohon fetch). Saita `targetTimeoutMs` a cikin combo,
tsoffin combo, ko provider override kawai don sa combo fallback ya yi sauri; ƙimomin da suka wuce
timeout na upstream na yanzu ana takaita su zuwa timeout na upstream.

`comboTimeoutMs` kasafin lokaci ne na daban (wall-clock) na dukan combo a fadin failover targets.
Bar shi ba a saita ba ko `0` don ci gaba da maimaitawa mara iyaka (dakatarwar hang-stop na minti 10 da aka hardcode a
`comboPredicates.ts` har yanzu yana aiki). Ƙima mai kyau tana maye gurbin wannan
hanyar kariya domin combo. Sa `comboTimeoutMs` ya fi `targetTimeoutMs` tsawo domin failover
ya sami lokaci bayan target na farko mai jinkiri.

### Matakain Circuit Breaker

Daidaitawar circuit breaker a matakin provider. Tsoffin ƙimomi suna nuna ƙimomin da aka ƙara girma da ake amfani da su tun v3.6 domin haɗi 500+.

| Canjin                                                        | Tsoho     | Fayil ɗin Tushe                      | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| ------------------------------------------------------------- | --------- | ------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |

| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_OAUTH_RESET_MS`                    | `60000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Tagar sake saita (ms) ga mai katse zagayen mai samar da OAuth.                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_API_KEY_THRESHOLD`                 | `12`      | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Iyakar gazawa a jere ga masu samar da maɓallin API.                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_API_KEY_RESET_MS`                  | `30000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Tagar sake saita (ms) ga mai katse zagayen mai samar da maɓallin API.                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_LOCAL_THRESHOLD`                   | `2`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Iyakar gazawa a jere ga masu samar da gida (Ollama, LM Studio, ...).                                                                                                                                                          |
