| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_LOCAL_FAILURE_WINDOW_MS`          | `300000`  | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Mai katse zagaye na matakin mai samarwa: tagar ƙidayar gazawa mai juyawa (ms) ga masu samar da gida.                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_LOCAL_COOLDOWN_MS`                | `60000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Mai katse zagaye na matakin mai samarwa: hutu (ms) da zarar an cika iyakar mai samar da gida.                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_COOLDOWN_MS`                               | `60000`   | `open-sse/services/apiKeyRotator.ts` | Hutun kowane maɓalli (ms) wanda juyawar maɓallan API da yawa ke aiwatarwa idan 429 daga sama (upstream) ba ya ɗauke da `Retry-After` (#14573). Ƙimar da ba ta wuce sifili ko ba ta zama lamba tana komawa zuwa tsohuwar ƙima. |
| `PIN_DROP_BACKOFF_LEVEL`                                      | `2`       | `open-sse/services/combo.ts`         | Zurfin backoff da ake ɗaukar mai samar da pin ɗin ma'ajiyar mahalli a matsayin mara lafiya na dindindin, sannan a jefar da pin ɗin domin sauyawa (failover).                                                                  |
| `PIN_DROP_GRACE_MS`                                           | `20000`   | `open-sse/services/combo.ts`         | Tagar hana girgiza (ms) da ke jure ɗan gajeren hutu na wucin gadi kafin a jefar da pin ɗin ma'ajiyar mahalli.                                                                                                                 |

### Yanayin amfani

| Yanayi                                      | Saiti                                                              |
| ------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| **Ƙirƙirar lamba mai ɗaukar tsawon lokaci** | `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS=900000` (minti 15)                             |
| **Takaita jimillar rayuwar raha (stream)**  | `STREAM_ACTIVE_TIMEOUT_MS=1260000` (minti 21)                      |
| **Gazawa da sauri ga API na samarwa**       | `API_BRIDGE_PROXY_TIMEOUT_MS=10000`                                |
| **Samfuran tunani mai faɗaɗɗewa**           | `STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS=300000` (minti 5 tsakanin gutsare-gutsare) |

## 16. Rajista

Tsarin rajista yana rubutawa zuwa stdout da kuma fayilolin rajista masu juyawa. Ana karanta dukkan saituna daga `src/lib/logEnv.ts`.

| Sauyi                                     | Tsoho                      | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| ----------------------------------------- | -------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `APP_LOG_LEVEL`                           | `info`                     | Mafi ƙarancin matakin rajista: `debug`, `info`, `warn`, `error`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `APP_LOG_FORMAT`                          | `text`                     | Tsarin fitarwa: `text` (mai sauƙin karantawa ga mutum) ko `json` (mai tsari).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `APP_LOG_TO_FILE`                         | `true`                     | Rubuta rajista zuwa fayil tare da stdout.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `APP_LOG_FILE_PATH`                       | `logs/application/app.log` | Hanyar fayil ɗin rajista (dangane da tushen aikin ko `DATA_DIR`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `APP_LOG_MAX_FILE_SIZE`                   | `50M`                      | Matsakaicin girman fayil kafin juyawa. Yana karɓar: `50M`, `1G`, `512K`, ko bayit kai tsaye.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `APP_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS`                  | `7`                        | Yawan kwanakin adana fayilolin rajistar manhaja da aka juya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `APP_LOG_MAX_FILES`                       | `20`                       | Matsakaicin adadin kwafin ajiyar fayilolin rajista da aka juya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CALL_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS`                 | `7`                        | Yawan kwanakin adana shigarwar rajistar buƙata/kira a cikin rumbun bayanai.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `CALL_LOG_MAX_ENTRIES`                    | `10000`                    | Matsakaicin adadin shigarwar rajistar kira a cikin ma'ajiyar ƙwaƙwalwar wucin gadi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CALL_LOGS_TABLE_MAX_ROWS`                | `100000`                   | Matsakaicin adadin layuka a teburin SQLite na `call_logs` kafin rage su.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `ENABLE_REQUEST_LOGS`                     | _(ba a saita ba)_          | Tilasta kunna ko kashe cikakken rajistar buƙata, tare da ƙetare saitin dashboard.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `MAX_PENDING_REQUEST_AGE_MS`              | `3600000` (awa 1)          | Matsakaicin shekarun shigarwar rajistar buƙata mai aiki da ta zama marar mai ita kafin tsaftace ƙwaƙwalwar wucin gadi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `CALL_LOG_PIPELINE_CAPTURE_STREAM_CHUNKS` | `false`                    | Adana gutsattsarin kwarara a cikin kayayyakin pipeline lokacin da `call_log_pipeline_enabled=true`. Dole ne a zaɓi kunnawa (`true`) — a kashe yake ta tsohuwa don adana sararin faifai.                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CALL_LOG_PIPELINE_MAX_SIZE_KB`           | `512`                      | Matsakaicin girman kayan rajistar kiran pipeline a KB lokacin da `call_log_pipeline_enabled=true`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `PROXY_LOGS_TABLE_MAX_ROWS`               | `100000`                   | Matsakaicin adadin layuka a teburin SQLite na `proxy_logs` kafin rage su.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `PROXY_LOG_INCLUDE_IPS`                   | `false`                    | Haɗa IP na abokin ciniki/fita da farkon asusu a cikin rajistocin console na `[ProxyEgress]`. Bayanan rajistar proxy na dashboard/rumbun bayanai suna riƙe da cikakkun bayanai.                                                                                                                                                             |
| `PROXY_LOG_FIRST_CHUNK_TIMING`            | `false`                    | Saita zuwa `"true"` ko `"1"` don yin rikodin `first_chunk_ms` (daga fara aikawa zuwa bayit na farko mai amfani na jiki) a layukan rajistar proxy. Yana naɗe kowane jikin upstream a cikin lazy passthrough stream sannan ya gyara layin da zarar bayit ɗin ya iso; a kashe yake ta tsohuwa domin martani su wuce ba tare da an taɓa su ba. |
| `APP_LOG_ROTATION_CHECK_INTERVAL_MS`      | `60000` (minti 1)          | Sau nawa `src/lib/logRotation.ts` ke sake duba girman fayil ɗin rajista mai aiki.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `CHAT_LOG_TEXT_LIMIT`                     | `65536`                    | Matsakaicin tsawon kirtani da ake riƙewa a cikin kayayyakin rajistar hira (tsoho 64 KB).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CHAT_LOG_ARRAY_TAIL_ITEMS`               | `128`                      | Adadin abubuwan jeri da ake riƙewa daga ƙarshen jeri lokacin gajarta payload na rajistar hira.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `CHAT_LOG_MAX_DEPTH`                      | `6`                        | Matsakaicin zurfin kafa-cikin-kafa kafin a gajarta payload na rajistar hira.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CHAT_LOG_MAX_OBJECT_KEYS`                | `80`                       | Matsakaicin adadin maɓallan object da ake riƙewa a cikin payload na rajistar hira (0 = babu iyaka).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CHAT_LOG_MAX_BODY_KB`                    | `1024`                     | Girman dukkan jikin buƙata/martani (KB) kafin a maye gurbinsa da taƙaitaccen bayani kawai maimakon cikakken kwafi. Ƙara wannan idan dogayen tattaunawar wakili suna nuna alamar riƙe wuri maimakon ainihin saƙonni a dashboard.                                                                                                            |
| `CHAT_DEBUG_FILE`                         | `false`                    | Lokacin da yake true, `serializeArtifactForStorage` yana tsallake gajartawa bisa girma. Don gyaran kurakurai kawai.                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |

## 17. Inganta Memory

| Variable                      | Default                                                                                         | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| ----------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB`         | _auto_ (bare metal); **`1024` a cikin hoton Docker**                                            | **Abu da ake ba da shawara** iyakar heap na V8 (MB) don Docker/standalone. Idan ba a saita ba, ana ƙaddara shi ta atomatik (~35% na RAM na tsarin, an takaita zuwa `[512, 4096]`); `512` shine mafi ƙaranci kawai idan ba a iya karanta jimillar memory ba. A kan `run-standalone.mjs` (Docker CMD), ana ƙara ƙima **take bayyana** a matsayin `--max-old-space-size` kuma **ta yi nasara** a kan tutar heap ta NODE_OPTIONS mai takaddama (V8 last-flag). `omniroute serve` har yanzu yana son heap na NODE_OPTIONS da ke akwai (#5238). Kada ku saita duka biyun zuwa lambobi daban-daban — tsarin yana rubuta gargaɗi yana ambaton duka ƙimomin biyu da wanda ya yi nasara. **Hoton Docker na hukuma koyaushe yana saita `1024`, don haka ƙaddarawa ba ta taɓa gudana a can ba.** Coding-agent `/v1/responses` yana buƙatar `8192`–`12288` tare da ƙarin wuri na cgroup — duba [Jagorar Docker — RAM na lokacin aiki](../guides/DOCKER_GUIDE.md#runtime-ram-for-coding-agents). |
| `OMNIROUTE_RSS_PRESSURE_MB`   | _auto_ (`max(3072, 2 × OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB)`; a kashe idan ba a saita `OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB` ba) | `open-sse/utils/resourcePressure.ts`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | Iyakar yanke aiki ta cikakken RSS na tsari (MB) don memory na native/waje da V8 `heapUsed` ba zai iya gani ba; sama da ita sababbin buƙatun suna samun 503 `resource_pressure`. RSS ba sau da yawa yana saukowa bayan ƙaruwa, don haka haɗa ta da `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_SELF_RESTART`. |
| `PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_SIZE`       | `50`                                                                                            | Mafi yawan shigarwar system prompt da aka adana a cache.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_BYTES`      | `2097152` (2 MB)                                                                                | Mafi yawan jimillar girman cache na prompt.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `PROMPT_CACHE_TTL_MS`         | `300000` (minti 5)                                                                              | TTL na shigarwar cache na prompt.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_SIZE`     | `100`                                                                                           | Mafi yawan amsoshi na temperature=0 da aka adana a cache.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_BYTES`    | `4194304` (4 MB)                                                                                | Mafi yawan jimillar girman semantic cache.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `SEMANTIC_CACHE_TTL_MS`       | `1800000` (minti 30)                                                                            | TTL na shigarwar semantic cache.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_CORPUS_CACHE_SIZE` | `5`                                                                                             | Tushen local-corpus da suke riƙe da index mai rai a cikin memory a lokaci ɗaya (`src/lib/localCorpus/configured.ts`). LRU: a iyaka ana fitar da index na tushen da ba a yi amfani da shi ba kwanan nan kuma ana sake gina shi a tambayarsa ta gaba. An takaita zuwa mafi ƙarancin `1`; ƙimar da ba lamba ba tana komawa zuwa na asali.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `STREAM_HISTORY_MAX`          | `50`                                                                                            | Mafi yawan abubuwan stream na kwanan nan a cikin buffer na kallo kai tsaye na Dashboard.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `CONTEXT_LENGTH_DEFAULT`      | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                                               | Musanya tsawon context na duniya (tokens) ga kowane mai samarwa da ba shi da mai canji `CONTEXT_LENGTH_<PROVIDER>`. Idan an saita, yana **maye** ƙimar catalog, musanya na dashboard da na registry ga waɗannan masu samarwa; idan ba a saita ba, OmniRoute yana amfani da sarƙaɗɗiyar hanyarsa ta ciki, wadda mafita ta ƙarshe ita ce `128000`. Duba [Tsawon context na kowane mai samarwa](#per-provider-context-length-context_length_provider).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `USAGE_TOKEN_BUFFER`          | `100`                                                                                           | Ƙarin tokens da aka keɓe lokacin bin diddigon iyakar amfani.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |

### Tsawon context na kowane mai samarwa (`CONTEXT_LENGTH_<PROVIDER>`)

`open-sse/services/contextManager.ts` (`getEnvOverride()` / `resolveTokenLimit()`) kuma yana karanta
nau'in **kowane mai samarwa** wanda ake gina sunansa daga ID na mai samarwa a lokacin aiki, don haka ba a iya
jera shi layi-layi a teburin da ke sama ko a `.env.example`.

**Sunan.** `CONTEXT_LENGTH_<PROVIDER>`, inda `<PROVIDER>` shine ID na mai bada sabis da aka rubuta da manyan haruffa, tare da maye gurbin kowace harafi da yake waje da `A-Z` / `0-9` da `_`. ID na mai bada sabis shine na asali bayan warware laƙabi (ɓangaren `provider` na jerin `provider/model` bayan an warware gajeren laƙabi). Nodes na musamman masu jituwa da OpenAI ko Anthropic suna amfani da ID na node da aka samar musu, don haka UUID yana cikin sunan canjin. Ƙimar tana zama cikakkiyar lamba mai kyau ta adadin tokens; duk wani abu dabam (fanko, `0`, mara kyau, wanda ba lamba ba) ana watsi da shi kuma warwarewa yana ƙaura zuwa mataki na gaba.

| ID na mai bada sabis                                        | Sunan canjin                                                               |
| ----------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| agentrouter                                                 | CONTEXT_LENGTH_AGENTROUTER                                                 |
| openrouter                                                  | CONTEXT_LENGTH_OPENROUTER                                                  |
| openai-compatible-chat-0f8e2c1a-5b7d-4e3f-9a6c-2d1b8e4f7a90 | CONTEXT_LENGTH_OPENAI_COMPATIBLE_CHAT_0F8E2C1A_5B7D_4E3F_9A6C_2D1B8E4F7A90 |

**Fifiko.** Ga ma'auriyar `provider/model`, mataki na farko da ya fitar da ƙima shine yake cin nasara:

1. `CONTEXT_LENGTH_<PROVIDER>` na wannan mai bada sabis.
2. `CONTEXT_LENGTH_DEFAULT` (maye gurbin muhalli na gaba ɗaya).
3. Tagar kowace samfuri: maye gurbin context da aka adana (da aka saita a dashboard, ko da aka kafa ta atomatik ta hannun discovery reconciler, duba `CONTEXT_WINDOW_RECONCILE_INTERVAL`), in ba haka ba ƙimar catalog / models.dev da aka daidaita.
4. `defaultContextLength` na registry na mai bada sabis.
5. Heuristics na sunan samfuri (`claude` 200000, `gemini` 1000000, `gpt` / `o1` / `o3` / `o4` /
   `codex` 400000).
6. Tsofaffin ƙimomin kowace mai bada sabis da aka gina a ciki, sannan `128000`.

Dukkanin matakan muhalli biyu suna shafar **dukkan mai bada sabis**: kowace samfuri na wannan mai bada sabis yana samun iyaka ɗaya, kuma suna cin nasara kan maye gurbin dashboard na kowace samfuri. Idan samfuri ɗaya kawai na mai bada sabis yana buƙatar wata taga dabam, a maimakon haka a yi amfani da maye gurbin kowace samfuri a dashboard.

**Yankin amfani.** Wannan jerin yana tafiyar da binciken tagar context a lokacin buƙata (ƙin karɓar `context_length_exceeded` / "Input exceeds context window" a
`open-sse/handlers/chatCore.ts`), kasafin kuɗin matsa prompt da iyakokin context na combo. `context_length` da `GET /v1/models` ke sanarwa har yanzu na iya fitowa daga metadata na catalog ko registry kuma ba a tabbatar da cewa yana nuna maye gurbin muhalli ba.

**Misali.** Mai bada sabis wanda samfurinsa yana karɓar tagar token 1,050,000 yayin da catalog yana bayar da 128,000:

```dotenv
CONTEXT_LENGTH_AGENTROUTER=1050000
```

A sake kunna uwar garken bayan canza shi: ana karanta ƙimar daga muhallin tsarin uwar garken.
A **manhajar tebur** uwar garken tana gudana a matsayin child process kuma ba ta ganin canjin tsarin da aka saita bayan an ƙaddamar da manhajar; a sa layin a fayil ɗin `.env` da manhajar take loda (tsarin bincike a
[Jagorar Electron: Binciken fayil ɗin muhalli](../guides/ELECTRON_GUIDE.md#environment-file-lookup)),
sannan a rufe manhajar gaba ɗaya kuma a sake ƙaddamar da ita.

### Matsawa

| Canji                                 | Tsoho         | Bayani                                                                                                                                   |
| ------------------------------------- | ------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_RTK_TRUST_PROJECT_FILTERS` | ba a saita ba | Amince da `.rtk/filters.json` na aikin ba tare da hash na `.rtk/trust.json` ba. Yi amfani da shi kawai a ci gaban gida da ake sarrafawa. |

### Injin Ƙwaƙwalwa (tsari 21)

### Farashin event-loop na ƙwaƙwalwa, ƙwarewa, da sabuntawar token (#10349)

OmniRoute **tsari Node ɗaya** ne. Cirewa/samowa ƙwaƙwalwa, sanya ƙwarewa, da sabuntawar token na mai bada sabis suna gudana a wannan **event loop ɗaya** tare da `GET /healthz` da dashboard. Ba worker thread ba ne.

| Aiki                      | Lambar                             | Tsoho                                               | Ikon mai sarrafa                                                                                                                                    |
| ------------------------- | ---------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Cirewa / samowa ƙwaƙwalwa | `src/lib/memory/`                  | Dashboard **memoryEnabled** (a kunne a tsoho)       | A kashe **Saituna → Ƙwaƙwalwa**. Babu wata hanyar kashewa ta env dabam fiye da kashe fasalin a saituna.                                             |
| Sanya ƙwarewa             | `src/lib/skills/injection.ts`      | Dashboard **skillsEnabled** (a kunne a tsoho)       | A kashe **Saituna → Ƙwaƙwalwa/Ƙwarewa** (`skillsEnabled`). Mabudin sandbox da ke ƙasa kawai suna iyakance aiwatarwa bayan an riga an kunna sanyawa. |
| Sabuntawar token          | `src/sse/services/tokenRefresh.ts` | A kunne ga masu bada sabis na OAuth/web da aka haɗa | A cire haɗin mai bada sabis ko a bar tokens su ci gaba da zama masu inganci; babu env `TOKEN_REFRESH=0` a yau.                                      |

Idan `/healthz` yana jinkiri a kwamfyuta mara aiki, a kashe ƙwaƙwalwa + ƙwarewa da farko, sannan a duba nauyin catalog/matsawa (#10303, #9685). Waɗannan fasaloli suna miƙa wuya a wuraren `await` amma har yanzu suna gasa kan zaren ɗaya.

Mabudin embedding layer, ma'ajiyar vector da sake matsarawa na ƙaramin tsarin ƙwaƙwalwa madawwami (`src/lib/memory/`).

| Mai canji                            | Tsoho                       | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| ------------------------------------ | --------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `MEMORY_EMBEDDING_CACHE_TTL_MS`      | `300000` (5 min)            | TTL na cache na embedding a cikin ƙwaƙwalwar ajiya (kowane sa hannun source/model/dim).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `MEMORY_EMBEDDING_CACHE_MAX`         | `1000`                      | Mafi yawan shigarwar LRU da ake kiyayewa a cikin cache na embedding.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `MEMORY_TRANSFORMERS_MODEL`          | `Xenova/all-MiniLM-L6-v2`   | ID na repo na HF don pipeline na MiniLM na gida na opt-in `@huggingface/transformers` (~23 MB int8, ~400 MB RAM).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `MEMORY_STATIC_MODEL`                | `minishlab/potion-base-8M`  | ID na repo na HF don embedder na lookup-table na static potion/Model2Vec. Ana saukar da shi lazily zuwa cikin kundin cache.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `MEMORY_STATIC_CACHE_DIR`            | `<DATA_DIR>/embeddings`     | Kundin da ake amfani da shi don adana fayilolin samfurin static potion. Tsoho yana ƙarƙashin `DATA_DIR` idan ba a saita ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `HF_HUB_ENDPOINT`                    | `https://huggingface.co`    | Maye gurbin URL na tushe na Hugging Face Hub da `staticPotion.ts` ke amfani da shi (misali endpoint na mirror don saitunan air-gapped).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `MEMORY_VEC_TOP_K`                   | `20`                        | Top-K tsoho da binciken vector na brute-force na `sqlite-vec` ke amfani da shi a cikin `src/lib/memory/vectorStore.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `MEMORY_RRF_K`                       | `60`                        | Madaidaitaccen `k` na Reciprocal Rank Fusion don karɓar hybrid FTS5 + vector (girke-girken sqlite-vec).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `VECTOR_STORE_DISABLE_VEC`           | `false`                     | Hanyar gwaji/gano matsala a cikin `getVectorStore()` (`src/lib/memory/vectorStore.ts`): idan `true`, yana tilastawa vector store ya zama `null` (yana kwaikwayon yanayin cloud/WASM ba tare da `sqlite-vec` ba), yana ragin karɓar ƙwaƙwalwa zuwa binciken mabuɗin kalma na FTS5. Bar shi ba a saita ba a production.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `NOTION_API_KEY`                     | _(ba a saita ba)_           | Mabuɗin API don backend na Notion (wanda preset na backend da aka sani na `genericBackend.ts` ke amfani da shi).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `NOTION_API_URL`                     | `https://api.notion.com/v1` | URL na tushe don API na Notion (ana iya maye gurbinsa don madadin Notion na self-hosted).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OBSIDIAN_API_KEY`                   | _(ba a saita ba)_           | Mabuɗin API don backend na Obsidian Vault (wanda preset na backend da aka sani na `genericBackend.ts` ke amfani da shi).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OBSIDIAN_API_URL`                   | `http://localhost:27123`    | URL na tushe don API na Obsidian Vault (ana iya maye gurbinsa don vault mai nisa).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_ENABLED`         | `false`                     | Babban canjin TV6 typed memory decay. **Opt-in (tsoho a kashe)** — sweep yana **share** ƙwaƙwalwar da suka lalace. Idan an kashe shi, `access_count`/`last_accessed_at` su ne telemetry kawai kuma ba a taɓa share komai.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_EPISODIC_DAYS`   | `30`                        | TTL (kwanaki) da bayansa ƙwaƙwalwar `episodic` da ba a yi amfani da ita ba take lalacewa. `0` yana sanya episodic ma ta zama mai rigakafi. Nau'ikan da suka dore (`factual`/`procedural`/`semantic`) ko da yaushe suna da rigakafi. Agogon lalacewa yana sake kafa tushe a kan `last_accessed_at`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_ACCESS_IMMUNITY` | `3`                         | Ƙwaƙwalwar da aka saka `>=` wannan yawan sau take zama mai rigakafi ga lalacewa duk da nau'in. `0` yana kashe rigakafin samun dama.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_SWEEP_INTERVAL`  | `0` (an kashe)              | Tazara (daƙiƙa) don zaɓaɓɓen periodic decay sweep a cikin `src/lib/memory/typedDecay.ts`. `0`/ba a saita ba = babu periodic sweep. Opt-in ninki biyu: yana kuma buƙatar `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_ENABLED=true`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_STRICT_SYSTEM_PROVIDERS`  | _(ba a saita ba)_           | ID na masu samarwa da aka raba da waƙafi (ba su bambance manya da ƙananan haruffa) waɗanda suke karɓar saƙon `system` **a index 0 kawai** (`src/lib/memory/injection.ts`). Ga waɗannan, haɗe ƙwaƙwalwa a tsakiyar array mai aminci da cache ba lafiya yake a tattaunawar multi-turn, don haka ana haɗe ƙwaƙwalwar/sanya ta a gaba a matsayin jagorar saƙon system. Tsoho zuwa `xiaomi-mimo`/`mimo` kawai; faɗaɗa don ƙarshen da suka dace da OpenAI na self-hosted (misali Qwen3.5+/3.6) waɗanda samfurin taɗinsu yana tilasta irin wannan ƙuntatawar saƙon system guda ɗaya a gaba. |

### Misalin Docker na Ƙarancin RAM

`128` na dashboard ne kawai. Coding agents a kan wannan heap suna fuskantar `FATAL ERROR` a lokacin doguwar `/v1/responses`. Kada ka yi amfani da wannan misalin a matsayin ƙofar Claude/Codex/Grok.

```bash
OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB=128
PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_SIZE=20
PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_BYTES=524288        # 512 KB
SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_SIZE=25
SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_BYTES=1048576     # 1 MB
STREAM_HISTORY_MAX=10
```

| `PRICING_SYNC_INTERVAL` | `86400` (24h) | `src/lib/pricingSync.ts` | Tsakanin lokacin daidaitawa a cikin daƙiƙu. |
| `PRICING_SYNC_SOURCES`  | `litellm`     | `src/lib/pricingSync.ts` | Kafofin bayanai da aka raba da waƙafi.      |

## Daidaita Arena ELO

| Sauyi                        | Tsoho         | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                                                      | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| ---------------------------- | ------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `ARENA_ELO_SYNC_ENABLED`     | `true`        | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts`                                     | Daidaita ELO na jadawalin jagororin Arena AI lokaci-lokaci, wanda za a iya saita shi daga Tutocin Fasali na Dashboard ko amfani da `false` don ƙin shiga.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `MODELS_CATALOG_PREFIX_MODE` | `dual`        | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts`, `src/app/api/v1/models/catalog.ts` | Tsarin prefix da ake amfani da shi ga ids na samfura a cikin `GET /v1/models`. `dual` yana nuna duka gajeren alias prefix da canonical provider prefix ga kowane samfuri (dacewa da sigogin baya — yana kusan ninka girman kasidar); `alias` yana fitar da id ɗaya ga kowane samfuri; `canonical` yana fitar da cikakken provider-id prefix kawai (masu samarwa waɗanda alias ɗinsu ya riga ya zama canonical id suna riƙe shigarwa ɗaya tak). Abokan hulɗa za su iya sauya wannan ga kowace buƙata ta amfani da `?prefix=alias`. Duba [API_REFERENCE](API_REFERENCE.md#model-id-prefixes-prefix). |
| `ARENA_ELO_SYNC_INTERVAL`    | `86400` (24h) | `src/lib/arenaEloSync.ts`                                                            | Tsakanin lokacin daidaitawa a cikin daƙiƙu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |

## Mai Samar da Playground na PromptQL (Ba na Hukuma ba/Na Gwaji)

Gadar zaman GraphQL da aka sake gina ta hanyar nazarin tsarin prompt.ql.app (`src/shared/constants/providers/web-cookie.ts`). Dukansu na zaɓi ne — saitunan tsoho suna nuni zuwa endpoints na playground na jama'a; a sauya su kawai don tura PromptQL mai zaman kansa/wani dabam.

| Sauyi                        | Tsoho                                                              | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                                         | Bayani                                                                        |
| ---------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `PROMPTQL_GRAPHQL_ENDPOINT`  | `https://data.prompt.ql.app/promptql/playground-v2-hge/v1/graphql` | `open-sse/executors/promptql.ts`                                        | GraphQL endpoint da ake amfani da shi don ayyukan taɗi/zama.                  |
| `PROMPTQL_CREDITS_ENDPOINT`  | `https://data.pro.ql.app/v1/graphql`                               | `open-sse/executors/promptql.ts`, `open-sse/services/usage/promptql.ts` | GraphQL endpoint da ake amfani da shi don neman ma'aunin kuɗin amfani/amfani. |
| `PROMPTQL_TOKEN_REFRESH_URL` | `https://auth.pro.ql.app/ddn/project/token`                        | `open-sse/executors/promptql.ts`                                        | Endpoint da ake amfani da shi don sabunta token gwargwadon iyawa.             |
| `PROMPTQL_POLL_TIMEOUT_MS`   | `180000`                                                           | `open-sse/executors/promptql.ts`                                        | Matsakaicin lokaci (ms) na binciken `thread_events` kafin lokaci ya ƙare.     |

## Mai Bayar da HyperAgent na Yanar Gizo (Ba na Hukuma ba/Gwaji)

Gadar zama da aka gano ta hanyar nazarin baya don hyperagent.com (`src/shared/constants/providers/web-cookie.ts`). Na zaɓi ne — ƙimar tsohuwa tana nuni zuwa maƙurar biyan kuɗi/amfani ta jama'a; a sauya ta kawai don turawar HyperAgent da aka ɗauki nauyinta da kai ko wata madadiya.

| Sauyi                  | Ƙimar Tsohuwa                                       | Fayil ɗin Tushe                         | Bayani                                                                  |
| ---------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `HYPERAGENT_USAGE_URL` | `https://hyperagent.com/api/settings/billing/usage` | `open-sse/services/usage/hyperagent.ts` | Maƙurar da ake amfani da ita don ɗauko tubalan ƙimar biyan kuɗi/amfani. |

## Ƙayyadaddun Amfani na Kilo Code

Duba ma'aunin USD na mutum da amfanin Kilo Pass don mai bayar da Kilo Code. Na zaɓi ne — ƙimar tsohuwa tana nuni zuwa Kilo API na jama'a; a sauya ta kawai don hanyar isarwa ko tsarin gwaji. Tantancewa tana amfani da alamar shiga ta OAuth da haɗin yake da ita.

| Sauyi          | Ƙimar Tsohuwa         | Fayil ɗin Tushe                       | Bayani                                                                                      |
| -------------- | --------------------- | ------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `KILO_API_URL` | `https://api.kilo.ai` | `open-sse/services/usage/kilocode.ts` | Tushen URL da ake amfani da shi don ɗauko ma'aunin Kilo Code na mutum da amfanin Kilo Pass. |

## Mai Bayar da Adobe Firefly na Yanar Gizo (Ba na Hukuma ba/Gwaji)

Sabunta zama ta hanyar burauza don mai bayar da Adobe Firefly na yanar gizo
(`open-sse/services/adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts`, `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`,
`open-sse/services/adobeFireflyClient.ts`). Na zaɓi ne — an daidaita duk ƙimomin tsohuwa don shigarwar
kwamfutar tebur ta yau da kullum.

> **An cire a #9255.** Tsohon tsarin tafiyar Chrome mai haɗin CDP (adobeFireflyChromeRuntime.ts) an
> maye gurbinsa da sabis ɗin shiga ta burauza na Playwright, kuma saitunansa ba sa samuwa. Sauye-sauyen
> ADOBE_FIREFLY_CHROME_ CDP_PORT / VISIBLE / HEADED / PING / FORCE_RESTART, tare da
> ADOBE_FIREFLY_LOGIN_WAIT_MS da ADOBE_FIREFLY_FORTER_WAIT_MS, ba a karanta su a ko'ina cikin
> tushen lambar — saita su ba ya yin wani tasiri.

| Sauyi                                | Ƙimar Tsohuwa     | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                 | Bayani                                                                                                                                    |
| ------------------------------------ | ----------------- | ----------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_CHROME_HEADLESS`      | `0`               | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts` | Saita zuwa `1` don Chrome marar fuskar gani na gaske (an san cewa ba ya aiki don ƙirƙirawa; don gano matsala kawai).                      |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BROWSER_REFRESH`      | `1`               | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | Shiga/fita daga dumama burauza tun da wuri. `0` yana kashe dumama tun da wuri (farfaɗowa daga 408 a tsakiyar rukuni har yanzu yana aiki). |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_SESSION_DISK`         | `1`               | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | Saita zuwa `0` don kashe adana zaman Adobe Firefly a faifai.                                                                              |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_MIN_SUBMIT_GAP_MS`    | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | Mafi ƙarancin tazara (ms) da ake tilastawa tsakanin aika buƙatu a jere, yana maye gurbin ƙimar tsohuwa da aka gina a ciki.                |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BATCH_EXTRA_GAP_MS`   | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | Ƙarin tazara (ms) da ake ƙara bayan nasarar rukuni, yana maye gurbin ƙimar tsohuwa da aka gina a ciki.                                    |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_SUBMIT_BASE_DELAY_MS` | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyClient.ts`       | Jinkiri na tushe (ms) kafin aika buƙatar ƙirƙirawa, yana maye gurbin ƙimar tsohuwa da aka gina a ciki.                                    |

## 19. Daidaita Model (Dev)

| Maɓalli                             | Tsoho             | Fayil ɗin Tushe                    | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| ----------------------------------- | ----------------- | ---------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `MODELS_DEV_SYNC_ENABLED`           | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `src/lib/modelsDevSync.ts`         | Tilasta kunna ko kashe daidaita farashin models.dev. Idan ba a saita ba = a bi Saituna > AI (`modelsDevSyncEnabled`). `0`/`false`/`off`/`no` **yana rinjayar DB** kuma yana tsallake daidaitawa ta lokaci-lokaci da binciken SQL/JSON na `getModelsDevPricing()` (don farfaɗowa idan dashboard ya maƙale a kan event loop ɗaya). `1`/`true`/`on`/`yes` yana tilasta kunna daidaitawa. Ajiye/share farashi har yanzu suna kiran `backupDbFile("pre-write")`, wanda ba ya yin komai ƙarƙashin takurawar mintuna 60 ko `DISABLE_SQLITE_AUTO_BACKUP`. |
| `MODELS_DEV_SYNC_INTERVAL`          | `86400` (24h)     | `src/lib/modelsDevSync.ts`         | Tazarar daidaita kundin model a lokacin haɓakawa, cikin daƙiƙu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CONTEXT_WINDOW_RECONCILE_INTERVAL` | `86400` (24h)     | `src/lib/contextWindowResolver.ts` | Tazara (daƙiƙu) ga mai daidaita context-window mai gyara kansa (5004): yana liƙa windows da provider ya ayyana daga gano `/models` a matsayin sauye-sauyen `auto:discovery` idan sun bambanta da kundin. Saita zuwa `0` don kashewa. Yana sake amfani da bayanan da aka riga aka daidaita (ba sabon fetch); ba ya taɓa maye gurbin sauye-sauyen `manual`.                                                                                                                                                                                         |

Lokacin da aka saita shi zuwa lamba N mara mãrã, ana ba da ɓangaren tarihin amfani na `GET /api/cache` (jimlar prompt-cache + yanayin canji) daga cache na amsar nazari da aka raba idan ya ɗauki aƙalla N ms don gina, a kowace tagar `trendHours` da aka ƙuntata, har na `OMNIROUTE_ANALYTICS_CACHE_TTL_MS` (tarihi na iya jinkirta har zuwa wannan TTL; `DELETE /api/cache` yana soke shi). Data volume ɗinsa yana riƙe da takardun shaidar OAuth/API na provider, kuma hoton da aka kafa ba shi da wata hanyar canza `api-keys` ta data-plane daga muhalli (sai dai `config.yaml` da aka dora), don haka `0.0.0.0` yana fallasa sabis ɗin da ke ɗauke da takardun shaida ga dukkan LAN.                                                                                                                                                          |

| `OPENWA_SERVICE_PORT`                          | `8323`                                         | `src/lib/services/bootstrap.ts`                                                    | Sake saita tashar da daemon open-wa (sarrafa WhatsApp Web ta atomatik) da aka haɗa a ciki yake saurare (kullum 127.0.0.1).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `DARIO_HOST`                                   | `127.0.0.1`                                    | `open-sse/executors/dario.ts`                                                      | Mai masaukin bind/connect na sabis ɗin Dario da aka haɗa a ciki (loopback kawai ta hanyar tsoho).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `DARIO_PORT`                                   | `3456`                                         | `open-sse/executors/dario.ts`                                                      | Tashar sabis ɗin Dario da aka haɗa a ciki.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `DARIO_HOST`                                   | `127.0.0.1`                                    | `open-sse/executors/dario.ts`                                                      | Mai masaukin bind/connect na sabis ɗin Dario da aka haɗa a ciki (loopback kawai ta hanyar tsoho).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |

| `LOCAL_HOSTNAMES`                              | _(fanko)_                                      | `open-sse/config/providerRegistry.ts`                                              | Ƙarin sunayen masauki da aka raba da waƙafi waɗanda ake ɗauka a matsayin "na gida" (sunayen sabis na Docker, da dai sauransu).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |

`ENABLE_CC_COMPATIBLE_PROVIDER` an yi shi ne kawai don relays na ɓangare na uku waɗanda ke karɓar clients na Claude Code kawai. OmniRoute yana sake rubuta buƙatu domin waɗannan relays su karɓe su. Idan kana son amfani da Claude Code CLI kawai, ko kuma ba ka tabbatar da abin da waɗannan relays suke ba, ci gaba da barin wannan a kashe kuma ƙara wani provider na yau da kullun da ya jitu da Anthropic maimakon haka.

| `PROXY_HEALTH_UNHEALTHY_CACHE_TTL_MS`             | `2000`                      | `src/lib/proxyHealth.ts`                                                                   | TTL na cache na gwaje-gwajen lafiyar proxy da suka gaza. Riƙe wannan a gajere fiye da `PROXY_HEALTH_CACHE_TTL_MS` domin lokutan da proxy ke cika lokaci na wucin gadi a ƙarƙashin babban concurrency su sake gwadawa da sauri ba tare da kashe fast-fail ga proxy ɗin da suka mutu da gaske ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |

| `PROXY_HEALTH_RELAY_INTERVAL_MS`                  | `21600000`                  | `src/lib/proxyHealth/relayCadence.ts`                                                      | Tazarar bincike a ms ga proxies na edge-relay (`deno`/`vercel`/`cloudflare`), waɗanda binciken su ke tashe wani serverless isolate da ake biya (aƙalla `60000`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |

| `PROXY_HEALTH_AUTO_DEACTIVATE`                    | `false`                     | `src/lib/proxyHealth/statusPolicy.ts`                                                      | Idan `false` (na asali), binciken isa-wuri na atomatik (scheduler + maɓallin "Test All" na `/api/settings/proxies/auto-test`) **karanta-kawai** ne kuma ba su taɓa rubuta matsayin proxy ba — mai aiki kaɗai ne ke saita aiki/rashin-aiki, don haka bincike maras daidaito ba zai iya barin proxy da aka sanya a makare ba (#6246). Saita `true` don mayar da tsohon yanayin gwaji-da-saita.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `FLUSH_EMPTY_RETRY_ENABLED`                       | `false`                     | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`                                                         | Tutar fasali ta zaɓi (duba [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md); wani override na DB na dashboard yana cin nasara). `true` (ko `1`, `yes`) yana sake gwada zagayen streaming da aka fassara amma marasa abun ciki ta hanyar shaidar gama-gari (har zuwa `STREAM_RECOVERY.EMPTY_TURN_RETRY_MAX` na sake gwadawa) maimakon fitar da 200 fanko ko 502 marar-abun-ciki.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `EMPTY_STREAM_SIBLING_RECOVERY_ENABLED`           | `false`                     | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`                                                         | Tutar fasali ta zaɓi (duba [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md); wani override na DB na dashboard yana cin nasara). `true` (ko `1`, `yes`) yana kunna binciken abun ciki na stream-fanko na Claude/Codex kuma yana kashe sake gwadawa mai iyaka da yake akwai a kan wata haɗi da ta cancanta bayan zagayen `empty_content`, ba tare da cooldown ko kulle samfur ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |

Ba a taɓa amfani da shi don tsara hanya ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `PROXY_OPERATOR_EGRESS_ENABLED`                   | `false`                     | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`                                                         | Tutar fasali ta zabi (duba [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md); watsi da dangi na DB na dashboard yana cin nasara). `true` (ko `1`, `yes`) yana karɓar adiresoshin da aka lura tare da kwanan wata waɗanda mai aiki ya turawa ga kowane memba na pool kuma yana haɗa su da karatun journal don nuni da tsarin pool.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `PROXY_OPERATOR_EGRESS_WINDOW_HOURS`              | `24`                        | `src/lib/db/proxyOperatorEgress.ts`                                                        | Tagar lura da shi a awanni don jerin fita da mai aiki ya tura (ana karatunta a kowane karatu; ba a buƙatar sake farawa). An taƙaita zuwa 1..168; idan ta wuce iyaka, ba lamba ba, ko fanko, tana komawa zuwa 24 tare da gargadi ɗaya na duk tsari, ba a jefawa ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE`                               | `false`                     | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                         | Saita `true` don barin mai tsara ayyuka ya cire proxies ta atomatik bayan gazawar maimaitawa a jere.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE_AFTER`                         | `3`                         | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                         | Gazawu a jere kafin mai tsara ayyuka ya cire proxy ta atomatik (lokacin da `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE=true`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `PROXY_AUTO_DISABLE`                              | `false`                     | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                         | Saita `true` don barin mai tsara ayyuka ya kashe proxy ta sanyi (matsayi `dead`, ba a taɓa sharewa ba) bayan gazawar maimaitawa a jere, maimakon cire ta. Madadin da ba ya lalatawa ga `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE`: proxy yana fita daga warware pool/rotation nan take (tacewar matsayin rayuwa da warware scope-pool ke amfani da ita riga ta fitar da ita) kuma ana sake kunna ta ta atomatik da zarar ta fara wuce bincike. Tana raba ƙofar `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE_AFTER`. Idan duka tutoci biyu `true` ne, `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE` yana cin nasara.                                                                                                                                           |
